# TouchLine: game design

This document describes the game as implemented in `src/engine` and `src/game`. Every number quoted here is taken from the code at the time of writing; where a system is planned but not built it is listed under Roadmap at the end and nowhere else.

## 1. Name

**TouchLine** is the product name, written as one word with a capital L and never in capitals. It is the manager's domain (the one place a manager is allowed to stand), it is short and it is brandable: it is the `<title>`, the manifest name, the wordmark in the top bar and the first line of the share text. The repository, the npm package and the save keys keep the lower-case slug `touchline`, and "Touchline XI" is the game logic's fallback team name; the setup screens open with an invented one (section 3). The logo, the palette and the type are in [DESIGN_SYSTEM.md](DESIGN_SYSTEM.md).

Tagline: **Spin the wheel. Draft the XI. Manage the season.**

Alternatives considered and set aside:

| Name | Why not |
| --- | --- |
| Gaffer | Good voice, but it names the person rather than the game, and it is widely used. |
| Hotseat | Captures job security, but it reads as a quiz format and ignores the draft. |
| Dugout Dynasty | Describes only one of four modes and is long. |
| Centurion | The 100-point season is one achievement among thirty-five, not the whole promise. |
| Fortune XI | Leans on the luck of the wheel and undersells the management half. |

## 2. Pillars

1. **The wheel is the only way in.** You never pick from a list. Every player arrives through a spin that lands on a real club in a real season, so every squad is a story of where the wheel took you.
2. **Then you manage for real.** Roles, instructions, condition, morale, injuries, suspensions, a cup, a transfer window, a board with a target. Each system has a bounded, legible effect that the UI explains.
3. **Nostalgia with a spine of data.** Players are tied to a season and rated for how they played that year, from 2008-09 to the current 2026-27 campaign. Your drafted side takes one real club's place and plays the other nineteen clubs' real squads.
4. **Deterministic and shareable.** Every spin and every match flows from one seed, so a Daily Challenge is the same wheel for everyone and a seed can be sent to a friend. Every season ends in a share text.
5. **Legible randomness.** Luck exists (Poisson goals, variance scaled by consistency) but it is explained in match reports and tactical notes, never hidden.

## 3. Modes

| Mode | Draft | Season | Persistence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Quick Draft | 11 picks, 1 re-spin (normal difficulty; easy 3, hard 0), each club-season usable once; an optional bench of up to 5 more picks (Setup, default 1) | Squad complete with the pundits' prediction, then the season played back game by game (simulated up front, revealed at 1.5 s a game at 1x) with a live table and position chart; the goal is 38-0; a January window after matchweek 19 (toggle, default on); fixed XI apart from one January deal and, with a bench, three changes and injury cover (below), default roles, no injuries carried without a bench, no live state; an optional Continental Cup run after a top-four finish | Result to local leaderboard and share text |
| Daily Challenge | As Quick Draft, seed `daily-YYYY-MM-DD` (UTC), the day's league from `DAILY_EPOCHS` (the Premier League before 2026-10-30, then the seven leagues one a day), every rule pinned by `dailySettings` (its original 2 re-spins, ratings shown, Squad First, season ratings, twists on, all-time, no club or nation filter, no modes, no manager) | As Quick Draft; `DAILY_SEASON` pins the January window on and the Continental Cup run off | Streak, daily result, achievement at seven days running |
| Weekly Challenge | As Quick Draft, seed `weekly-YYYY-Www` (the ISO week, UTC, Monday to Sunday); the league, season, host, shape and one theme (a wheel rule) are fixed by the week | As Quick Draft | Its own streak and one result per week (`profile.weekly`), a leaderboard row like any quick season, achievement at four weeks running |
| Season | 16 to 22 picks (default 18), 3 re-spins (easy), club-seasons may repeat | 3 friendlies, 38 matchweeks played one at a time, cup, January window, board | Full save in `localStorage` |
| Dynasty | As Season, then a summer draft each year with bonus spins and 1 re-spin | Season mode repeated; players age, contracts run down, league moves on a year | History table per year |
| Arena: The Gauntlet | 14 picks, the quick re-spins and twists, a bonus pick after rungs 2, 4, 6 and 8 (up to 18) | A ladder of fifteen real club-seasons, each from a stronger band of the wheel, one match each until the first defeat; managed live, by the assistant, or Sim to the end (section 17) | Best runs table and the cabinet's Gauntlet shelf, the arena's save slot |
| Daily Arena | The Gauntlet's or the Cup's, seed `arena-YYYY-MM-DD` (UTC), England wheel, twists on, no filters | The Gauntlet on even dates, the Cup of 16 against the wheel on odd ones; everyone meets the same opponents (section 17) | Best runs, its own streak and one result per day (`profile.arenaDaily`), achievement at seven days running; never the Daily's leaderboard or streak; in an online build, a run the assistant played posts to the World leaderboard's Daily Arena board (docs/ONLINE.md section 5g) |
| Run-in | The host's real squad of 18, then 4 spins on its league's wheel, 1 re-spin, no twists | Take over at matchweek 28 of 38 (25 of 34) in the table projected from the real final one, with a brief (the title, the top four, survival); the last ten matchweeks of the Season loop, Realistic, job security on, no cup, window, Europe or friendlies (section 10, "The Run-in") | Run-ins table and the cabinet's run-in shelf, its own save slot; never the leaderboard |
| Arena: The Cup | 16 picks, the quick re-spins and twists, whatever the size | A knockout of 8, 16 or 32 real club-seasons (seeded draws, four days between ties, bans that carry), or a one-off final; each tie managed live through the two stops and a shoot-out, played by the assistant, or Sim to the end (section 17) | Best runs table and the cabinet's arena cups by size, the arena's save slot |

Setup options (`src/ui/screens/Setup.tsx`), in 38-0's order as numbered steps (a circled number and a short title each) in two boxes, at most 1480px wide. Left box: **1 Pick your formation**, **2 Name your team**, **3 Choose your league**; right box: **4 Set the rules**, **5 Spin**. From 1000px wide the boxes sit side by side, and on a desktop window at least 600px tall the page fits the screen with no page scroll (checked at 1280x720, 1440x900, 2000x920 and 2560x1440 for every mode): the page fills the height under the top bar, step 1's pitch takes the height left in the left box, and with More options open the rules scroll inside the right box while Spin stays at its foot. Below 1000px the boxes stack, so the steps run 1 to 5 down the page, and at 720px or narrower Spin is the fixed bottom bar. On a desktop the choice cards show their titles only (the line is the tooltip and stays for screen readers). The Daily and a challenge link number their steps 1 formation, 2 name, 3 the day's or the link's rules, 4 Spin.

1. **Title and subtitle**: the mode's name and one line ("Spin real club-seasons, draft an XI, then watch its season play out." for Quick Draft).
2. **Formation** (step 1): the eight shapes (section 4) as tiles, two to a row beside the chosen shape on a desktop (picture beside the name), below it on a phone; the chosen one is drawn on a read-only `Pitch` with an empty dot per slot labelled by position (`.pitch-mini`), with its one-line description (`Formation.blurb`). A name with a word after the numbers puts the word on its own smaller line on a tile ("4-4-2" over "Diamond"), and the numbers never break at a hyphen.
   **Team name** (step 2): the `TeamNameField` (28 characters, from which a 2 to 3 letter code is derived; an invented name has its own three letters) with **Clear** and **Shuffle** (below). **League and season** (step 3): the league flags, and on the step's title line a compact season select (`data-testid="season-select"`, newest first, defaulting as before; the classic seasons 1992-93 to 2007-08 are listed, tagged est., only while Classic is on), the season the game simulates; the league season is no longer in the fold; for Season and Dynasty the line "You replace: <club>" with its badge and a **Change** link that opens More options at the club select.
3. **Difficulty**: three cards, Easy "3 re-spins", Normal "1 re-spin", Hard "No re-spins, ratings hidden" (the counts read from `DIFFICULTY_RESPINS`). Quick Draft opens on Normal, Season and Dynasty on Easy.
4. **Show ratings**: two cards, On and Off ("Blind: go on names and positions"). On Hard both are disabled, Off is shown chosen and one line says "Hard always hides ratings."; back on Easy or Normal the card's own choice returns.
5. **Draft mode**: Squad First ("Spin a club, draft anyone, then place him") and Position First ("Choose a slot, then spin for a club to fill it"), section 5.
6. **Player ratings** (Quick Draft only): Season ("Rated as he was in that very season") and Prime ("Every player at his career best"), Prime by default. Season and Dynasty always use season ratings, since their players age and develop from the real season.
7. **Era**: preset chips All-time, 2000s+, 2010s+, Modern (2016+) (`ERA_PRESETS`), a two-handle slider over the seasons the wheel leagues hold (`wheelSeasons`, 2008-09 to 2026-27 for England: 19 stops), a chip with "N of M seasons" (`eraCount`) and one caption line ("The wheel lands on 2016-17 to 2026-27."). A chip puts the handles where `eraHandles` says (All-time and 2000s+ at both ends with today's data, 2010s+ at 2010-11, Modern at 2016-17, the right handle on the newest season); moving a handle (drag or arrow keys; either handle stops at the other) switches to a Custom chip. The handles step through the list of seasons, not through the years (`seasonStop` snaps a year to the nearest season held, the earlier on a tie), so a gap in older data is never a stop and the slider can never select a range with no seasons. The formation preview is a picture, not a control: it is `inert`, so its slot discs are out of the tab order.
8. **Advanced** (Quick Draft; **More options** for Season and Dynasty): folded by default, remembered per browser (below). Toggles first: European Nights (the wheel and the market draw from every league; shown when two or more leagues exist), twists, and for Quick Draft the January window toggle ("January window: one deal at the halfway mark", on by default, `GameSettings.january`); for Season and Dynasty the Continental Cup (a Quick Draft offers its cups after the league instead, so Setup no longer shows it the toggle). Then the team name (28 characters, from which a 2 to 3 letter code is derived; Season and Dynasty keep it above the fold in a "Your squad" panel with the squad-size slider 16 to 22 and the job-security toggle), the seed (shareable, `XXXX-XXXX`), the league played in (for Quick Draft, also the Super League, below), the league season (2008-09 to 2026-27, the in-progress one flagged), the club whose place you take (you inherit their colours, rivals and fixture slot; their real players sit the season out), the opposition (**Calibrated**, the default, or **Realistic**, section 13), one-club mode and one-nation mode (section 5; choosing one clears the other).
9. **Spin** (step 5): the summary line (for a Quick Draft `quickSetupLine`: the team, the shape and the rules or modes only, "Touchline XI · 4-3-3 · Normal · bench 5 · Crazy", never the league season or the seed, the full `summariseSetup` line as its tooltip; Season, Dynasty, the Daily and a challenge keep `summariseSetup`) and the button, **Start Draft** (Season and Dynasty: "Start drafting your squad"; a challenge: "Take on Minh"), both in the fixed phone bar.

Every finished season's summary tells its Season Story (section 12): the quick result stores `clinchedAt`, `doomedAt` and `moment` with its game-by-game `results`; Season and Dynasty retell it from the fixtures.

The cards, the slider and the toggles become rules through `setupRules(base, choices)` (`src/game/logic/setup.ts`), a pure function: the base is `QUICK_RULES`, or `SEASON_RULES` with the squad size; `withDifficulty` sets the re-spins and keeps `hardMode` in step; `showRatings` is the card's choice except on hard, where it is false; `draftMode` and `ratings` are copied; the two handles are ordered and clamped to the wheel's seasons, and a handle at either end writes no bound, so a full slider is the All-time wheel and a range running to the newest season leaves `toSeason` out. Every preset therefore gives the same bounds as `eraRange`, except that 2000s+ (which starts before the data) writes none at all instead of `fromSeason: 2008`, the same wheel. An untouched Quick Draft setup is `QUICK_RULES` with the league plus Prime ratings, a bench of one (`benchPicks: 1`), the manager spin (`managerSpin: true`) and the end case (`endCase: true`), exactly what `firstDraftSettings` sends (tested); `DAILY_RULES` is `QUICK_RULES` but for its 2 re-spins and no manager (`managers: false`). The Daily and a challenge link show no cards: the team name, the day's or the link's notice, the formation preview of the fixed shape and the fixed rules as chips from `describeRules` (`2 re-spins`, `Ratings on`, `Squad First`, `Season ratings`, `All-time`, `Twists on` for the Daily: a re-spin budget that is not its difficulty's own is stated without the difficulty's name); `describeRuleChips` gives the same words with a picture id for the modes, the Upgrader, the power-ups, the manager and the end case, which Setup draws as the Lucide icon of the card that sets it (`RULE_ICON` in `components/icons.tsx`), never an emoji).

Note on Daily: `dailySettings(u, seed)` (`src/game/logic/daily.ts`) fixes everything but the team name: the day's league (`dailyLeagueOf`, below: the Premier League until 2026-10-29) as the wheel and the league, a complete season of it and a host club rotated by the date (`n = YYYYMMDD`, season index `n mod seasons`, host index `7n mod clubs`), the formation (`3n mod 5` over 4-3-3, 4-4-2, 4-2-3-1, 3-5-2, 4-4-2 Diamond), Calibrated difficulty and `DAILY_RULES`, which writes out every draft rule as a literal, spreading nothing from `QUICK_RULES` or `DIFFICULTY_RESPINS` (11 picks, the Daily's original 2 re-spins with the difficulty labelled normal, ratings shown, Squad First, season ratings, twists on, each club-season once, `accomplished` fit, no era, club or nation filter), so a later change to the quick defaults cannot make two players' Dailies differ; a test fails when `QUICK_RULES` gains a key `DAILY_RULES` does not pin. The 38-0 branch had moved the Daily to Normal's 1 re-spin; the owner chose to keep 2, so `DAILY_RULES` is back to the rules the Daily had before the 38-0 options (`respins: 2`, twists on, season ratings, Squad First, no modes), and `dailyPin.test.ts` plays four dates (spin, both re-spins, the assistant's picks, the season) under `DAILY_RULES` and under the literal pre-38-0 rules and asserts the same spins, XI and season, plus the pre-38-0 date mapping for six dates.

### Two-tap start

The setup screen (`src/ui/screens/Setup.tsx`) starts a game in two taps (the mode card, then Start Draft): a first visit opens every card on the mode's defaults, and since round 10 a Quick Draft, Season or Dynasty setup opens on the choices this viewer last started a draft with (`src/ui/setupMemory.ts`: per-viewer keys `touchline-setup-quick`, `-season`, `-dynasty`; every card, toggle and select but the seed, the team name, one club or nation and the season; read field by field, anything damaged taking the default; never a challenge link's, the Daily's or the Weekly's rules, and never in the save or a seed). While they differ from the defaults the line above Start ends with a **defaults** link (`setup-reset`) that puts every card back and forgets them. Above the fold sit the formation, the team name, the league, the 38-0 cards and the era, plus the squad-size slider and "You replace" for Season and Dynasty. Everything else (job security, European Nights, twists, the January window, the seed, the league season, the host club for Season and Dynasty, the opposition, one-club and one-nation modes, the Continental Cup) sits under the **Advanced** fold (named **More options** for Season and Dynasty), collapsed by default. Its state is remembered per browser in `localStorage['touchline-setup-advanced']` (`'1'` open, `'0'` closed, read and written inside try/catch so a private window simply starts closed). The Daily and a challenge link show no fold, since nothing in it is theirs to change.

Directly above the Start button a summary line, built by `summariseSetup` (`src/game/logic/setup.ts`) from the exact settings Start would send, states every choice: `Touchline XI, 4-3-3, Premier League 2025-26, calibrated, seed K7Q2-MX9A` (the club replaced is never named in it, nor in any header: the Draft, Kick-off, the season playback, the Dugout, the Summary's banner and share card, and the share text's second line `Touchline XI, 2025-26`), followed only by what is off the defaults (a European Nights wheel, an era, one club or nation, the 38-0 cards that differ from the mode's base rules through `rulesTags`: `easy mode`, `normal mode` (a season, whose default is easy) or `hard mode`, `blind`, `Position First`, `prime ratings`; then `twists` or `no twists` when they differ from the mode's default of on for a quick draft and off for a season, the squad size, no sack, the Continental Cup; for a quick draft `no January window` when it is off and `Continental Cup run` when it is on). A `change` link opens the fold and scrolls to it. On screens of 720px or narrower the line and the button sit in a fixed bottom bar (`.bottom-bar`, padded for the safe-area inset); the page's bottom padding is the bar's measured height plus 20px (150px until it is measured), so a long summary never covers the last panel and Start is reachable without scrolling past the formation grid.

**The look of every setup (Quick Draft, the Daily, Season, Dynasty, Head-to-head).** Few words, pictures and chips, on the redesign tokens (`SetupBits.tsx`):

- Formations are main's `FormationPicker` tiles (one tile style everywhere; the position-coloured dots of `formationThumb`), beside the chosen shape's pitch preview in step 1. The Daily, a challenge link and Crazy show only the pitch preview of the one shape they play, titled "Your formation", Crazy with a line saying the seed draws it.
- Leagues are a segmented row of drawn flags (`LeagueFlag`, never emoji) and the real league name, in step 3. The options are icon cards (`ChoiceCards` with `icon`; on a desktop the icon sits beside the title and the line is the tooltip). Each section has at most one short help line; details sit in Info tips (`SectionHead`, `InfoTip`), the Modes tip listing every mode's line. On a phone the mode cards show only the icon and name, four to a row.
- The Modes row opens step 4: the seven mode cards, then Power-ups, End case, Moneyball and Teammate chain as cards in the same style, eleven in all; on a desktop they are small cards, icon over name, wrapping onto more rows in a narrower window, the label kept for screen readers and its Info tip beside the row. Under 1000 px (round 10) they are one grid, two to a row at 390 px and one at larger text: each card its icon, its whole name and its one line (two at most), so every mode and switch says what it does without a tap and no name breaks mid-word; the count beside Modes counts the four switches too.
- Step 5 shows the one-line `summariseSetup` line above Start. `setupChips` (`src/game/logic/setup.ts`) still builds the same choices as short chips (the shape, the league and season, never the club replaced, the opposition and the seed, then what is off the defaults) for any screen that wants them; the setup itself shows the line.
- Every team name field (and each head-to-head manager's name) is a `TeamNameField` with **Clear** (empties the field and focuses it) and **Shuffle** (`team-name-shuffle`, a Lucide shuffle icon), another invented club name from `generateTeamName` (`src/game/logic/teamNames.ts`): a made-up place ("Ashvale", "Kingsmoor", "Lowfield", "Lantern Hill") with a common club word (United, Rovers, Athletic, Albion, Wanderers...), or `AFC Lowfield`, `Sporting Ashvale`, `Redmere FC`, `St Oswin Town`; tests check over 10,000 draws that none is a club in any pack and that no place is a word of a real club or a real town with a club of note. Setup (every mode), the arena's setup, an arena challenge and Home's first draft open with one (`useTeamName`, `src/ui/teamName.ts`), a fresh one each time, unless this viewer typed a name of their own and started a game with it: that name is kept in the per-viewer key `touchline-team-name` and prefilled next time (starting with an invented name clears it). An invented name's code is its place's first three letters (ASH; ST and the saint's initial for a St name); a typed name keeps `shortNameFor`'s initials. The names use the browser's own randomness and never touch the seed; a name is not part of any ranked settings hash. Shuffle never lands on the name in the field, nor in Head-to-head on the other manager's. An empty field starts as the invented spare shown as its placeholder (Manager 1 or 2 in Head-to-head); the game logic's own fallback stays Touchline XI.

The default host club is `defaultHostClub`: the club whose position in `u.tables[leagueId][season]` is nearest `DEFAULT_HOST_POSITION` (10), ties to the higher place, among the clubs with a squad that season; where the table is missing it is the alphabetical middle of the list, as before. The help text says why (`A mid-table club (10th in 2025-26), so the board's target is reachable`) until the player picks another club, and the rivals count is the league size minus one rather than a fixed 19, so an 18-club league reads as 17 rivals.

The Daily's fixed settings are `dailySettings(u, seed)` (in `src/game/logic/daily.ts`, re-exported from `src/game/logic/setup.ts`): the digits of `daily-YYYY-MM-DD` form `n`; the season is the complete English seasons in ascending order at `n mod count`, the host club is that season's club ids sorted at `(7n) mod count`, and the formation is `['4-3-3', '4-4-2', '4-2-3-1', '3-5-2', '4-4-2-diamond']` at `(3n) mod 5`; the league is England, the wheel England only, the difficulty calibrated, and the draft rules are `DAILY_RULES`. This is the mapping the setup screen always used, now pinned by a test (`daily-2026-10-25` is a 4-3-3 as Swansea City in 2017-18), so a deploy never changes everyone's puzzle.

**The league rotates in dated epochs** (2026-10-28, after engine 7 shipped; no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump: a date's puzzle is fixed by the date, and no date before the new epoch moves). `DAILY_EPOCHS` works like the Weekly's `WEEKLY_EPOCHS`: an epoch is a first day and a literal list of leagues, and from that day the Daily's league is `leagues[days since the first day mod length]` (`dailyLeagueOf`, from the seed alone). The first epoch (no first day) is the original Daily, `['england']`, and covers every date before the next one and any seed that names no date; the second starts on **2026-10-30** with the seven leagues in `leagues.json` order, `england, spain, italy, germany, france, netherlands, portugal`, so each league has its own weekday: Friday the Premier League, Saturday La Liga, Sunday Serie A, Monday the Bundesliga, Tuesday Ligue 1, Wednesday the Eredivisie and Thursday the Primeira Liga. The season, the host and the shape are then the same formula over the day's league (its complete, standard seasons; for France, the Netherlands and Portugal, which have no tables, every season before the newest; the wheel weights are flat there, as in any Quick Draft of those leagues), the wheel is that league alone (`rules.leagues`, `wheelLeagues`), and `DAILY_RULES` and `DAILY_SEASON` are untouched (the Weekly takes them too). A Premier League day of the rotation is exactly the original mapping. The first fortnight: 30 Oct England (Leicester City 2022-23, 4-3-3), 31 Oct Spain (Sevilla 2023-24, 3-5-2), 1 Nov Italy (Inter 2021-22, 3-5-2), 2 Nov Germany (Köln 2022-23, 4-4-2), 3 Nov France (Rennes 2023-24, 4-4-2 Diamond), 4 Nov Netherlands (FC Twente 2024-25, 4-2-3-1), 5 Nov Portugal (Nacional 2025-26, 4-3-3), then the same seven leagues from 6 Nov. A new rotation (a league added to the data, another order) opens a new epoch from a future day; a shipped epoch is never edited, and the lists are literal so a league appended to `leagues.json` moves no day. `dailyPlan(seed)` gives the league, the wheel and the shape before any pack is loaded: Home's card shows the day's flag and shape (and the season once the league is in), the Daily setup loads the day's league on arrival (`ensureLeagues`, plus a ranked pin's league if it names another) and says "loading the league…" until it is in, and offline with a league never downloaded Start reads "Not downloaded: connect to load it" with Retry (the league gate, as for the Weekly). `dailySettings` throws rather than build a Daily on another league's data; `dailyLoaded(loadedLeagues, seed)` says whether it can be built. Tests: `dailyRotation.test.ts` (every date from 2024-01-01 to 2026-10-29 byte for byte against a literal copy of the old function, with England alone and all seven loaded; the first fortnight's leagues; nine future puzzles and six assistant runs pinned; the same settings and the same season whichever other leagues are loaded; a year of rotated days drafting without a dry wheel or a classic season), `src/online/__tests__/dailyRotation.test.ts` (the ranked replay of a rotated day), and `dailyPin.test.ts` keeps its four dates and literals, read on the Premier League mapping (`dailySettings(u, seed, 'england')`, a test-only argument) since three of them are rotated days now. `dailyGameSettings` builds the full `GameSettings` from it, which is what the Daily setup's **Start Draft** sends to `startGame`. On Home, the Daily card's body and its **Play today's puzzle** button both open that setup (the team name, today's formation shown as given, the rules as chips, the seed, Start Draft); neither starts the draft by itself. If today's Daily is already under way the button reads **Continue today's puzzle** and reopens it.

### First run

The first ten minutes of a player who has never heard of 38-0 (`src/game/logic/onboarding.ts`, pure, no draws, no save field):

- **Who is new.** `isNewcomer(profile, gameWaiting)`: nothing finished in this browser (`resultsFinished`: the larger of the leaderboard, `seasonsSimulated` and the Daily results, plus arena runs, run-ins and head-to-heads; 0) and no game in progress or parked. A first draft abandoned before its season still counts as new; any result, from any mode, ends it for good. An older save with any result never sees it.
- **Home.** For a newcomer the hero's figures and flags give way to the game in three steps (`FIRST_RUN_STEPS`: Spin, the wheel lands on a real club in a real season; Draft, take one player from that squad, eleven spins make your XI; Play, your XI plays the whole season, can it win all 38?) and one button, **Play your first draft** (`data-testid="first-draft"`), with "About 5 minutes, no sign-up" and How it works beside it. The button starts a Quick Draft at once on `firstDraftSettings(u, randomSeed(), name)` with an invented team name (or the one this viewer typed before, `startingTeamName`): what an untouched Setup sends (the Premier League's newest complete season, its mid-table club replaced, a 4-3-3, Normal, ratings on, Squad First, Prime ratings, all-time, twists, the January window) with only the core loop (r8, 2026-10-28): no bench, so eleven spins make the XI as the first step says, no manager spin and no end-of-draft case (the manager is settled for you at kick-off, as Setup's Manager pick does); tested against the line Setup would show, "Touchline XI · 4-3-3 · Normal · Prime ratings". The extras stay one Setup screen away from the second draft on. Measured from a fresh browser with the first player offered each spin (Playwright, the full reel): see the change log of docs/HANDOVER.md for the before and after figures. The Daily card's button is a plain one for a newcomer, so Home has one filled button. The four mode cards stay; the More modes row (the Daily Arena, the Weekly, the Cup, the Gauntlet and the Run-in) is left off a newcomer's Home and comes in with the first result (r9), so the first screen holds the first draft and four choices, not nine. On a desktop the steps sit beside the lockup (up to 900 px tall; the button beside the steps up to 760 px; from 1100 to 1279 px wide, where the top bar takes two rows, the steps take the whole hero), so Home still fits one screen from 1100x700 to 2560x1440; a phone stacks them.
- **The first spin.** In a newcomer's draft, one line under Spin the wheel on a phone ("The wheel lands on a real club in a real season. Then you pick one of its players for your XI."; a desktop's middle box already says it). The `draft-first-reveal` coach mark then says what to tap. On the slot choice the bench is a plain button, so the slot chips are the call (it was the filled one, and a first tap benched the first pick).
- **Squad complete on a phone.** Simulate season is sticky at the foot of the prediction's box below 1100 px wide (it was about 1,000 px down at 390x844).
- **The first result.** `firstResultNext(profile, mode, dailyPlayedToday, cupWaiting)`: after the first quick-like result ever, once no post-season cup is still on offer, the summary shows **Your first season is in** (`components/FirstNext.tsx`, `data-testid="first-next"`) under the cups: today's Daily ("Everyone gets the same spins today, so your score compares", left out when that was the Daily or today's is played) and Set up a Quick Draft (where the formations, leagues and modes are). The tier gets the gold flare (`celebrationFor(tier, first)`, the fanfare with it) whatever the finish, and a **Your first season** chip (`first-season`) above it. Every later summary is unchanged.
- **What next, on Home.** `nextUpFor(profile, { dailyPlayedToday, online, seasonWaiting })` marks one card with **Try next** (`next-up`, an accent border) for a player with one to `NEXT_UP_UNTIL` (6) results: the Daily card until a Daily is played; then, on an online build, the World leaderboard tile once ("Try next: see where your Daily ranks", while two results at most are in); then the Season card until a Season or Dynasty is on the leaderboard, never while one is already waiting on the resume strip (r9: the marker sent a player with a Season under way to start another). While the Daily card is marked, the hero's "Today's Daily is waiting" nudge stands down, so the Daily is pointed at once; a streak at risk or a Daily played still shows. Home's next-badge line says what is left in the badge's own words ("Next badge: Serial Winner, 1 league title to go"; it read "0 of 1 for bronze"). Nothing is saved; a newcomer and everyone past six results see no marker.

#### Today and this week

Home keeps its four mode cards (Quick Draft, Daily, Season, Dynasty) and carries the casual modes as one row of five compact tiles under them, labelled More modes (`.home-tiles.casual`, `data-testid="casual-row"`, so Home still fits one screen): the Daily Arena (`arena-daily-card`), the Weekly Challenge (`weekly-card`), the Cup (`arena-cup-card`), the Gauntlet (`arena-gauntlet-card`) and the Run-in (`runin-card`). Each tile is an icon, a title and one meta line (today's arena or this week's theme, then the result and the streak once played):

- **Daily Challenge**, as above: its card and its button both open the Daily's setup to name the team; a Daily under way (active, or parked by an arena run or a Weekly) continues.
- **Games waiting**: the game in progress and each parked one get a resume strip above the mode cards (except today's Daily, whose card carries See the result and Clear, or Continue and Abandon), led by the mode ("Season · Touchline XI", "Quick draft (parked) · Touchline XI") over where it stands (`resumeStatus`, `src/game/logic/resume.ts`: Drafting, Kick-off, January window, Matchweek 12 of 38 · 2025-26, an arena run's own line, Season complete). On a desktop two or more strips share one row, so three waiting games no longer squeeze the mode cards.
- **Daily Arena** (`src/game/logic/arenaDaily.ts`): "Today: The Gauntlet on the Premier League wheel, a 4-2-3-1", then `Played: 6 rungs · streak 3` once played. One tap plays it (`dailyArenaGameSettings`, under the manager's own team name from `startingTeam` in `src/ui/teamName.ts`, the one last typed or an invented one, since r9; `puzzleOf` drops the names, so the puzzle and its board are the same); a run of today's arena already under way, active or parked, continues; any other unfinished arena run is dropped only on a yes; once today's is played, a second tap asks first, since a replay changes neither the day's result nor the streak. `dailyArenaSettings(seed)` is pure and frozen: with `n` the digits of `arena-YYYY-MM-DD`, an even `n` is the Gauntlet and an odd one the Cup of 16 against the wheel (so the format follows the day's parity: over the two years from 2026-10-25, 373 Cup days of 730, a format repeating on 15 days, at month ends, never three days running); an England wheel, `DAILY_FORMATIONS[(7n) mod 5]`, calibrated, twists on, no era, club or nation filter. Pinned: `arena-2026-10-25` the Cup in a 4-3-3, `arena-2026-10-26` the Gauntlet in a 4-2-3-1, `arena-2027-01-01` the Cup in a 4-2-3-1. The arena's seed streams (section 15) give everyone the day's ladder or draw whatever they draft. The share text's mode line reads `Daily Arena 2026-10-26 · The Gauntlet` and it prints no seed line; Play again on the summary starts an ordinary run on a new seed, off the streak (`arenaAgainSettings`).
- **Weekly Challenge** (`src/game/logic/weekly.ts`, mode `weekly`): "2026-W44 · European Nights in La Liga 2008-09 · ends Sunday" (the season appears once the week's league is loaded), then `Played: 62 pts, MID-TABLE · streak 2`. One tap loads the week's packs (`startWeekly` in the store: one league, or all seven on a European Nights week) and starts it; the card's body opens Setup on the week (`fixed.kind = 'weekly'`, a banner naming the theme, only the team name free). It is quick-like (`isQuickLike`), so the draft ends in the quick season, in the quick save slot. `weeklySettings(u, seed)` is pure and frozen; with `n = year × 100 + week`: the league is `leagues.json` order at `n mod 7` (only the first seven are ever indexed, so an appended league never moves a week), the season is that league's seasons with complete squads, ascending, at `(7n) mod count`, the host is that season's club ids sorted at `(3n) mod count`, the shape `DAILY_FORMATIONS[(11n) mod 5]`; it is null until the packs it needs are loaded. Pinned: `weekly-2026-W43` the Teammate chain in a 3-5-2 as Liverpool in the Premier League 2019-20; `weekly-2026-W53` European Nights in a 3-5-2 as Hoffenheim in the Bundesliga 2017-18; `weekly-2027-W01` the early years in a 4-4-2 as Catania in Serie A 2011-12.
  - **The Weekly's Setup record** (round 5; `src/game/logic/weeklyRecord.ts`, pure; `components/WeeklyRecordBox.tsx`): under This week's rules, one line on why the theme is worth a week, this week's counted result (the first run, the one the streak counts) and the best replay from the leaderboard, the weekly streak (alive while this week or last week counted; "play by Sunday" until this week does), last week's result and the four weeks before. It reads `Profile.weekly` and the leaderboard; nothing new is saved.
- **Themes** (`src/game/logic/weeklyThemes.ts`, append-only): `european-nights` (all seven leagues on the wheel), `early` (2008-09 to 2013-14), `modern` (2020-21 onwards), `hard` (hard mode), `realistic` (no calibration), `park-the-bus` (a 5-3-2 on Realistic), `one-nation` (the nation with the most players among those deep enough for a one-nation XI of the week's shape in the week's league, any season, other than the league country's own; ties by code), `moneyball` (a £180M cap) and `teammate-chain`. `position-first` is not a theme yet; it would join in an epoch of its own from a future week. `WEEKLY_EPOCHS` freezes the order: the first epoch runs from `weekly-2026-W43` and gives one theme a week in the order teammate chain, European Nights, early years, Moneyball, hard mode, one nation, modern game, park the bus, Realistic, then again; a new theme opens a new epoch from a future week, so no past week changes. Measured with the assistant's draft and quick season over 108 weeks from 2026-W43: 49.2 points a season and no title (teammate chain 41.6, European Nights 50.7, early years 53.5, Moneyball 51.7, hard mode 53.5, one nation 39.8, modern game 58.3, park the bus 49.8, Realistic 44.3). Most of the gap to an England quick draft (about 68) is the league: a plain quick draft by the assistant scores about 64 in Spain or Italy, 50 in the Bundesliga and 42 in the Eredivisie or the Primeira Liga.
- **Recording.** `recordResult` files a weekly result like any quick season (the leaderboard row, the cabinet's league totals, Best seasons under Quick and Daily) and also in `profile.weekly` (`{ streak, last, results }`, through `fileStreakResult` in `src/game/logic/streaks.ts`, so the week of the seed counts, the streak grows only from the week before, and a second result in a week changes nothing); it never touches `dailyResults`. A Daily Arena run files Best runs and the cabinet's arena shelves as any run, and its day in `profile.arenaDaily` (`{ streak, last, results: { format, score, label } }`), never a leaderboard row or the Daily's streak. In an online build the finished run is also filed for the World leaderboard's casual boards (a Daily Arena the assistant played, every Weekly Challenge that stayed inside its theme's rules), posted once signed in and replayed by the server (docs/ONLINE.md section 5g); nothing in the game changes. The cabinet counts weeks played (`weeklies`, the Sunday League badge) and Daily Arena days (`arenaDailies`). Achievements: `arena-regular` (Arena Regular, seven Daily Arenas running) and `weekly-streak-4` (Month of Sundays, four weeks running). The summaries say where the run stands (r9, `streakStanding` in `src/game/logic/casualStreak.ts`, display only): "Today's Daily Arena counts · 3 days in a row · a new one at midnight (UTC)" (`arena-daily-standing`) or "This week's Weekly counts · …" (`weekly-standing`) when the book's result for the seed is this run's, else "A practice run: today's counted result stays …".

### Coach marks and the Key

Fourteen coach marks (`TIP_IDS` in `src/game/logic/tips.ts`) explain a screen or mechanic the first time it is met, one at a time (r8): eight for the core loop, then six (r9, 2026-10-29) that introduce round 8's features, each only at its moment. `tipInTurn(profile, mounted, finished)` shows the first tip on screen in `TIP_IDS` order that is not dismissed and is due (`TIP_AFTER`: the results a profile must have finished, `resultsFinished`; the core tips are due from the start), and a second waits until the first is dismissed or leaves the screen; a tip not yet due neither shows nor holds back the next. So the first draft and its season carry the core tips alone, and no feature tip lands on the first result's summary, which already has its celebration, its first badges and the next steps. Beside Got it, **No more tips** (`tip-no-more`, `store.dismissAllTips`) dismisses them all at once; Show tips again brings them back. Each is a `Tip` (`src/ui/components/bits.tsx`): a `.tip` callout inline in the page flow, with an accent left border, one or two sentences and a **Got it** button. It is never an overlay and never disables a button, so a player who ignores it loses nothing. Got it calls `store.dismissTip(id)`, which records the ISO date in `profile.seenTips[id]` (an optional save field persisted with the profile; an older save has none, so every tip is unseen, and a second dismissal keeps the first date). The core eight, in the order a new manager meets them:

| id | where | text |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `draft-first-reveal` | above the landed squad, while the XI is being filled | Tap a player to draft him. The number is his rating: higher is better. Green is his own position, amber one he can cover for a few points less. |
| `draft-bench` | under the needs panel once the XI is full (and the wheel has not run dry) | Bench picks can be anyone un-drafted; cover every position, injuries will come. |
| `draft-recap` | in the draft recap under its grade, once every pick is made (not when no pick is graded) | Each pick has a grade: A took the best man his spin offered for an open slot, D left the most on the wheel. The Hindsight XI is the best side your spins held. |
| `dugout-first` | top of the Squad tab | Kick off plays your next match: Watch it live, let the assistant Auto-play it, or Sim straight to the score. A disc is his rating in that slot. (The bars, face and rings are the Key chip's.) |
| `tactics-first` | top of the Tactics tab | A role moves a player's quality between units; rating in role is what the engine uses, and the team rating updates as you change it. |
| `halftime-first` | inside the half-time panel (not at the 70th minute) | A talk, four instructions and up to three substitutions; No changes plays on. |
| `report-first` | above the match report's tactical notes | xG is the chances each side created; the notes are the exact modifiers your instructions produced. |
| `market-first` | above the window's listings | Bids near the asking price are accepted, bids well under it annoy the club and raise the price; the asking price shows what it was on day one. |

The feature tips (r9), with when each may first show:

| id | where | due | text |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `daily-rotation` | the Daily's setup, above its steps (in view on a phone before Start), on a day whose league tomorrow differs (`dailyRotation(today, tomorrow)` in `onboarding.ts`; none before the rotation's first full day, 2026-10-30) | from the start | The Daily's league changes every day: today's wheel is the Premier League, tomorrow's La Liga. Everyone gets the same spins. |
| `office-first` | the Office tab, and the Inbox's office strip when an item first lands there | from the start | The Office is where your players come to you: requests to leave, promises you made, one-to-one talks, the press conference before each match and fee talks in a window. Nothing here changes a result until you use it. |
| `share-first` | the summary's banner, above Challenge a friend, Copy result and the share card | 2 results | Challenge a friend sends a link to your exact spins (a Season: the result with a link to TouchLine) and the card asks them to beat it. |
| `feats-first` | the trophy unlock card on a summary | 2 results | Badges, milestones and feats are kept in the Trophy Room; Home names the next one to chase. |
| `explore-first` | Explore, under its view switch | 1 result | Every real player and club-season the wheel can land on: search, a club's page with its chance on the wheel, compare. |
| `dock-first` | Home on a phone held upright, where the nav is the dock (`(max-width: 720px) and (min-height: 501px)`), above the mode cards; never a newcomer's Home | 1 result | Head-to-head, Explore, your Profile and the guide are in the bar along the bottom. |

The copy stays qualitative so it cannot drift when the engine is tuned. **Show tips again** in the Manager panel of the Profile screen (`store.resetTips`, which empties `seenTips`) brings them all back, and the panel says how many are dismissed.

The Squad panel's header carries a **Key** chip. On, it prints `Cond`, `Mor` and `Shp` under every player's three state bars (`PlayerRow`'s `showLabels`) and a one-line legend under the pitch (`PitchLegend`): the disc is his rating in that slot; a white ring is natural, amber `acc.` is accomplished (position factor 0.86 or more, a small cost), red `awk.` is awkward or worse (below 0.86, a big cost), and a red ring is also injured or suspended. The pitch now draws its rings from `describeFit` in `src/engine/positions.ts`, the same bands as before, so the legend and the discs cannot disagree. The fit colour is the lowest-priority ring (`fitRingColour` in `src/ui/format.ts`): an injured or suspended player shows the red ring wherever he plays, and the selected (gold) and eligible (green) highlights also win over it, while the `acc.` or `awk.` label under the disc still names the fit. The Key is a per-viewer convenience, kept in `localStorage['touchline-squad-key']` (`'1'` on) inside try/catch; blocked storage reads as off and the chip still works for the visit.

## 4. Formations

Eight shapes, each with eleven slots carrying a position and pitch coordinates (`src/engine/formations.ts`):

| Formation | Slots |
| --- | --- |
| 4-3-3 | GK, LB, 2 CB, RB, DM, 2 CM, LW, ST, RW |
| 4-4-2 | GK, LB, 2 CB, RB, LW (as LM), 2 CM, RW (as RM), 2 ST |
| 4-2-3-1 | GK, LB, 2 CB, RB, 2 DM, LW, AM, RW, ST |
| 3-5-2 | GK, 3 CB, LB and RB (as wing-backs), DM, 2 CM, 2 ST |
| 3-4-3 | GK, 3 CB, LB, RB, 2 CM, LW, ST, RW |
| 4-1-4-1 | GK, LB, 2 CB, RB, DM, LW, 2 CM, RW, ST |
| 4-4-2 Diamond | GK, LB, 2 CB, RB, DM, 2 CM, AM, 2 ST |
| 5-3-2 | GK, LB, 3 CB, RB, 2 CM, DM, 2 ST |

Every formation chooser (Quick Draft, Season and Dynasty setup, each manager in Head-to-head, the Dugout's Tactics tab) is the same `FormationPicker` (`src/ui/components/FormationPicker.tsx`): a tile per shape with a small drawn pitch, attack pointing up, and the eleven slots as dots placed by `formationThumb` (`src/game/logic/formationThumb.ts`), a linear scaling of the slot coordinates above into a 60 by 88 viewBox with a 6-unit inset. Dots are coloured by the slot's position group (goalkeeper gold, defenders blue, midfielders white, forwards orange), so a 4-4-2's wide men show as wingers because that is who the draft fills them with. The chosen tile has an accent border and a brighter pitch and reports `aria-pressed`; the picture itself is hidden from screen readers. Tests check that no two dots touch in any shape at radius 3.4, so every shape reads at phone width, where the tiles sit four to a row at 390 px (three on a 320 px phone) and the Tactics tab's tiles with blurbs sit two to a row. No images, no RNG.

Wide midfield slots are typed as LW/RW and wing-back slots as LB/RB, so position eligibility (section 6) decides who can fill them. Changing shape mid-season keeps roles whose position still matches, resets the rest to the default role and knocks 12 points off every player's familiarity.

## 5. The wheel and the draft

Rules (`src/engine/draft.ts`):

- **No dead spins.** The wheel pool is every club-season (within the era and club filters) that still contains at least one usable player. While the XI is being filled, usable means a player who can cover an open slot at the minimum fit (`accomplished`, section 6). Once the XI is full, any un-drafted human is usable for the bench.
- **A human is drafted once.** `takenPlayers` holds player ids (not player-season ids), so the same person cannot be drafted from a different club or season.
- **Quick drafts never repeat a club-season** (`uniqueClubSeasons: true`); season drafts may.
- **Spin weighting.** A season is picked uniformly from those still in the pool, so no era dominates because it has more rows. Within that season a club-season is weighted by `quality × usefulness`, where `quality = 1 + 0.06 × (21 − finishing position)` (1.6 if the season is in progress) and `usefulness = 1 + min(10, usable players) / 10`. A title winner is roughly twice as likely as a relegated side.
- **Re-spins** are a draft-wide budget set by the difficulty (quick: easy 3, normal 1, hard 0; season: 3; 1 in a dynasty summer; 0 in head-to-head). A re-spin logs the skipped club-season in the draft history. The Draft's button says what is left ("Re-spin · 1 left", "No re-spins left"; `respin`), its tooltip that a re-spin swaps the club-season and costs no pick. Once a player is tapped, "Where does he play?" (the slot chips, "Left CM 82 · fits", amber with the points a cover costs) scrolls into view however far down the list he was, and on a phone the landed squad opens with the club's name at the top of the screen (round 10, display only).
- **Picks** go into an open slot the player can cover, or onto the bench once the XI is full. The UI suggests the assistant's slot for him (`suggestSlot`, below).
- **Auto-complete** lets the assistant finish (`assistantChoice`): on each spin it takes the man with the best `rating × positionFactor` in the slot `bestSlotFor` gives him, or the highest-rated player for the bench. **Keepers** (engine 6, item 3): a squad of `FULL_SQUAD` (16) places or more gets at least `MIN_KEEPERS` (2) and never more than `MAX_KEEPERS` (3), any squad at most 3; while the bench is short of its keepers the assistant takes the best keeper a spin offers, passes on a spin with none when no pick is left to spare, and takes no keeper past three (a Dynasty summer counts the keepers it already holds through `playerLookup`). Engine 5 drafted one keeper in 18 often enough that an outfielder went in goal at the first injury, and Dynasty summers reached six or seven keepers in 23. `bestSlotFor` takes, of the open slots he may fill, his best positional fit (a natural slot always before a cover), then the position with the fewest open slots left (a DM/CM fills the lone DM and leaves the two CMs to the many who play there), then formation order. The head-to-head assistant (`h2hAutoPick`) makes the same choice. Engine `'4'` and before charged every cover a flat 0.92 and the head-to-head assistant took the highest overall into the first eligible slot in formation order, so a DM/CM went to centre-back with both central midfield slots open (TL-04): over 120 assistant head-to-head drafts, 57.9 per cent of XI slots held a cover at a mean positional cost of 7.1 per cent; now 0.8 and 0.08 (`assistantFit.test.ts`, which also checks the assistant never takes a cover while a natural slot for the man is open, over 160 head-to-heads and 160 Quick Drafts).
- In Dynasty, agent events (section 10) and the summer grant **bonus spins**, shown as a gold chip and redeemed at the summer draft.

### The 38-0 options: difficulty, ratings, draft mode, prime and era

Four optional `DraftRules` fields, each read through a helper so a save or a link written before them keeps its meaning, plus era presets over the existing `fromSeason` / `toSeason`.

- **Difficulty** (`difficulty: 'easy' | 'normal' | 'hard'`, `difficultyOf`): `DIFFICULTY_RESPINS` is 3, 1 and 0 re-spins. `withDifficulty(rules, d)` sets the budget, keeps the older `hardMode` flag in step (true only on hard) and forces ratings off on hard. `QUICK_RULES` is normal (1 re-spin, down from the 2 quick drafts had before difficulties), `SEASON_RULES` easy (3, as before). A rule set with `hardMode: true` reads as hard whatever else it says; without the field it reads as normal. The hard-mode toggle at setup now means `withDifficulty(base, 'hard')`, and the Blindfolded achievement is won on a hard draft.
- **Show ratings** (`showRatings`, default true; `ratingsHidden(rules)` is hard, `hardMode` or the toggle off): off is blind mode. It is always off on hard. Blind mode hides a rating only while it is being chosen: "once he's in the squad (you picked him) you can see his rating". The one rule is `ratingShown(rules, place, { twist, complete })` in `src/game/logic/ratingVisibility.ts` (pure, tested; `ratingHidden` is its inverse for the `hideRatings`/`hidden` props), and every screen that hid ratings asks it. Still hidden (`?`) in blind mode: the players a spin offers and the slot chips before the pick (`offer`), an Icon from the end-of-draft case before he comes in (`offer`), the January window's deals and their gain in the slot (`market`), and the challenge ghost chip (`ghost`). Shown once he is yours (`squad`, always true): the pitch, the bench and his draft-log row in the Draft, the Upgrader, armband and lock sheets (they list only drafted players), kick-off (the Squad complete bars, the pitch, the bench, captain and penalties order), the season playback's bench, the substitution board, the arena summary and both share cards, the head-to-head pitches. The pick grades (`grades`) show once the draft is complete, in blind mode too, since nothing is left to choose. A pick made without seeing his rating plays a small reveal as it lands: the number flips in on his pitch disc and his log row (`revealsOnPick`, `rating-reveal`, off under reduced motion). Hard keeps the same rule: its blindness is about choosing ("Ratings stay hidden while you draft"), so seeing your own squad changes no pick already made, and the Blindfolded achievement still needs a hard draft. Display only: nothing in the engine or the game reads it, it needed no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump, and the Daily and challenge links are unchanged.
- **Draft mode** (`draftMode: 'squad' | 'position'`, default squad). Squad First is the draft as it was: spin, then pick anyone usable and choose his slot. **Position First**: while the XI is being filled the player first chooses an open slot (`chooseSlot(u, d, slotId)`, stored as `DraftState.target`; no RNG), and `spin` refuses to run without one ("Choose a slot first"). The rules pool then counts as usable only players who can cover that slot (`canUse` and `eligibleSlots` narrow to the target), so the wheel, the spin weighting, the twists' candidate check and `canSpin` all see a pool of club-seasons with at least one undrafted player for that slot, era, club, nation and league rules applied: no dead spins. `pick` refuses any other slot ("This spin fills the CB slot you chose"), and the target clears with the pick. A re-spin keeps the slot, and the re-spun row records it (`history[].target`), as does the row "Auto-complete" skips. The one-club-season-once rule falls back as before (used club-seasons reopen when none is fresh), so a one-club wheel never empties for a slot it can fill at all; a slot nobody left can fill drops out of `spinnableSlots` and `chooseSlot` refuses it ("No club left on the wheel can fill GK"). `canSpin` before a slot is chosen is "some open slot is spinnable", so a dry one-nation wheel is still reported as dry. Bench picks, once the XI is full, are Squad First. The assistant keeps a slot already chosen and otherwise takes the first spinnable open slot in formation order. Tested over 40 seeds and four shapes with twists on, with one club (Burnley from 2020-21) and with one nation (Brazil, England and Spain). Squad First takes the same RNG draws as before, so `ENGINE_VERSION` did not move (it stayed `'3'`); Position First is a new rule and has no older seeds to keep. On the Draft screen, before each spin the open slots that can be filled pulse on the pitch and the spin button reads "Choose a slot" (disabled); tapping one marks it gold and the button reads "Spin for CB" (the slot's position); once the reel lands, every player in the list who can fill that slot is outlined in the accent colour with a "fits CB" or "can cover CB" chip (`.prow.fit`), and tapping one drafts him straight into it.
- **Player ratings** (`ratings: 'season' | 'prime'`, default season in `QUICK_RULES`; Setup's Quick Draft starts on Prime). Prime (`src/engine/prime.ts`) rates every player at his career best: his highest `overall` over every player-season of his `playerId` in the draft's wheel leagues (`rules.leagues`; every league in the universe when it is unset), ties to the earlier season, then the smaller player-season id, with that season's attributes. The wheel leagues, not the loaded ones: leagues load lazily, so a device that once opened La Liga holds it and another does not, and a prime over the loaded packs would rate the same England draft differently on the two and break challenge replays; the wheel leagues are always loaded for the draft, so the same rules rate everyone alike (tested: an England wheel rates every player the same with or without La Liga loaded beside it). A player with a single season in those leagues keeps that season's rating. One pass over the wheel leagues' club-seasons, cached per universe and league set in a WeakMap: 8 ms for England's 11,166 player-seasons (3,647 players), 65 ms for all seven leagues' 69,155 (19,096 players); 41,884 of those player-seasons are lifted, by 3.96 points on average and up to 29. A drafted player-season keeps its id, club, season, age, positions (slot eligibility was decided on them) and traits and takes the prime overall and attributes; `playerLookup` returns it that way under Prime, so the draft list, the pitch, team strength and the quick season all rate him there, and the assistant drafts by career-best rating. The real clubs he plays against keep their season ratings. Game-created players (Dynasty progression, free agents) are never re-rated.
- **Era presets** (`src/engine/era.ts`): `wheelSeasons(u, leagues)` lists the wheel leagues' seasons (the setup slider's stops) and `seasonStop(seasons, year)` the stop a year rests on and `eraHandles(preset, first, last)` where a preset puts the slider's handles; `eraRange(preset, first, last, custom?)` maps All-time to no bounds, 2000s+ to `fromSeason` 2000 clamped to the data's first season (2008-09 today, so it equals All-time), 2010s+ to 2010, Modern (2016+) to 2016, and a custom from/to to an ordered range clamped to the data. `eraCount(u, leagues, range)` gives the setup line: with England, All-time and 2000s+ are "19 of 19 seasons", 2010s+ "17 of 19", Modern "11 of 19" with the caption "2016-17 to 2026-27".
- **Labels.** `rulesTags(rules, base)` names what differs from the mode's defaults (`QUICK_RULES` for quick and Daily, `SEASON_RULES` otherwise): `Easy` or `Hard`, `Blind` (ratings off but not hard), `Position First`, `Prime ratings`. The Draft header, the summary line under the tier banner and a share-text line (`Hard · Position First · Prime ratings`) show them; default rules add nothing.
- **Older saves.** `normaliseRules` (`src/game/logic/save.ts`, applied to `settings.rules` and `draft.rules`) reads the difficulty from `hardMode` (hard) or the saved re-spin budget (3 or more easy, else normal), shows ratings unless hard, and fills Squad First and season ratings; the saved budget and `respinsLeft` are left alone.
- **Links.** The four fields travel in the rules diff and are checked by value; `rulesDiff` always writes `difficulty`, even at the default (`{ difficulty: 'normal' }` for default rules). A diff without a difficulty therefore comes from a link built before difficulties, when every quick draft had 2 re-spins and left `respins` out: `rulesFromPayload` opens it with `LEGACY_QUICK_RESPINS = 2` (unless the link wrote a budget out) and reads its `hardMode: true` as hard, so the friend gets the re-spins the challenger had. The replay ignores the budget, so verification is unchanged either way. Under Position First a row that drafted no one carries the slot it was filling, `[null, 'cb1']`, and the replay chooses each row's slot before its spin.
### Classic seasons (1992-93 to 2007-08)

The Premier League's sixteen seasons before the EA data starts ship as classic club-seasons (`cs.classic`, `cs.estimated`): real squads and real tables, with TouchLine's estimated ratings (data in `docs/DATA.md` section 8). They are **opt-in**: `rulesPool` skips them unless `DraftRules.classic` is true, so a draft, a challenge link or a save made without the rule spins exactly as before. They sit after every standard club-season in `u.clubSeasons` (`buildLeague` appends them, `mergeUniverses` puts every league's standard club-seasons first), so the weighted index of a standard club-season never moves. Every other pool follows the same rule: the market (`MarketContext.classic`), friendlies and the youth intake take classic players only for a game with `rules.classic`; the One-Nation scan only with `EraRange.classic`; the Continental Cup field, job offers and the dynasty's next season only in a classic league season (`isClassicSeason`); the Daily never (`dailySettings` filters them, which changes none of its output: `classicGate.test.ts` pins the first 60 Daily seeds and 200 seeded drafts to hashes captured before the gate). `RULE_CHECKS.classic` is a boolean, so a challenge link carries the rule.

- **Choosing them (Setup).** One toggle in the Era panel, under the slider: "Classic 1992-93 to 2007-08" with the compact "est." chip (`data-testid="era-classic"`), shown only when a wheel league has classic seasons (`classicFromFor`; England today) and never on the Daily or a challenge. Turning it on extends the slider's stops to 1992-93 (`wheelSeasons(u, leagues, true)`; "N of M seasons" counts them too, `eraCount` with `classic`) and puts the handles on 1992-93 and 2007-08; `setupRules` then adds `classic: true` (`SetupChoices.classic`), and the slider can widen the range into the standard seasons or run it to All-time. Off, the slider, the presets and the rules are exactly what they were, with no `classic` key. The league season select lists classic seasons too, tagged "(estimated ratings)" (`leagueSeasonChoices`): a side can play 1992-93 (22 clubs, 42 games) with a standard or a classic wheel. The setup line names "classic seasons (estimated ratings)", and a challenge banner says so.
- **Features after the gate.** Prime ratings count a classic season as a player's best only when the game opts in (`primeOf(u, id, leagues, classic)`, cached per league set and opt-in); the quick season's January listings skip classic club-seasons unless `rules.classic` (without this gate the Daily, which has the January window, would have changed); the reel's tiers rank standard and classic club-seasons apart (`tierTable`), so no standard club-season changes tier. `classicRelease.test.ts` plays seeded quick seasons (plain, Prime, bench, Position First, hard, Europe, an era) and four Dailies on England's standard pack alone and with the classic pack loaded, and requires the same game.
- **Labels.** Every estimated club-season and player-season shows an "Estimated ratings" chip (`EstimatedChip`, `data-testid="estimated-chip"`, a dashed neutral chip; "est." in a player row): the Draft's landed club-season, every `PlayerRow` (Draft, Explore, the Dugout), Explore's season header and club header, its highest-rated list and its search, and the career table. Explore's season select now starts at 1992-93 for England (classic seasons tagged "(est.)", with a one-line note over the table), and the career chart draws estimated seasons striped and fainter, with "estimated" in the tooltip. The reel's result line carries the compact chip beside the tier once it lands on a classic club-season. `isEstimated(u, ref)` decides from the club-season a player-season came from: pass the player-season itself where it is at hand (a dynasty's aged copy, `${playerId}@dyn${year}`, keeps its `clubId` and `season`, so it keeps the mark into 2008-09 and later); a bare id is read from the universe or its `${playerId}@${season}@${clubId}` shape. The share text gains a line from `estimatedNote` ("Estimated ratings: 3 classic picks", "…, a classic league season") and the share card's footer ends "· estimated ratings" when the XI or the league season is estimated; nothing changes for a standard result. "How it works" explains the estimate. The head-to-head season picker and the Dugout search still leave classic seasons out unless the game opted in.
- **Head-to-head (hot-seat).** The setup's league panel has the same toggle, "Classic 1992-93 to 2007-08" with the "est." chip (`data-testid="h2h-classic"`), shown when the league or the wheel leagues hold classic seasons (`classicFromIndex`). On, the classic pack loads (Start waits for it), the season select (newest first) adds the classic league seasons tagged "(est.)" (`h2hSeasonChoices`, `src/game/logic/h2hClassic.ts`), and `startH2H` takes `h2hRulesFor(wheelLeagues, hardMode, true)`: `h2hRules` plus `classic: true`, so the engine's own `rulesPool` lets the wheel land on classic club-seasons. Off, the rules object is exactly `h2hRules`'s (no `classic` key), and `h2hClassic.test.ts` pins 124 seeded head-to-heads (24 with their seasons) to hashes from release-e6, on the standard pack alone and with the classic pack loaded; 200 seeded drafts with it on never run the wheel dry, and a 1993-94 league season (22 clubs, 42 games) plays through. The draft screen shows a "Classic seasons" chip, the landed club-season's "est." chip and the player rows' own; the result adds "Estimated ratings: …" (`estimatedNote`) to the share text and "estimated ratings" to the cards. A classic head-to-head reloads with the classic pack (`h2hNeedsClassic` in the store's `loadData`), and the screen waits for its packs with Retry instead of rendering a half-loaded universe. Online rooms stay classic-off: they take `h2hRules` directly and the server's universe never loads the classic pack.

### The Super League

A Quick Draft can be played in the **Super League** instead of a real league season (`DraftRules.superLeague`, optional, read through `isSuperLeague` in `src/game/logic/superLeague.ts`, which also needs `mode === 'quick'`, so a managed season or the Daily never plays it; challenge links play as quick drafts and carry it). The field is in `src/engine/superLeague.ts`:

- **The nineteen.** Every club-season with at least eleven players in the pool (`poolClubSeasons(u, classic)`: classic seasons only with `rules.classic`) is built as an AI side exactly as a league season builds one (`aiClubOf`: the club's style and shape, every player fresh) and rated by `aiStrength`: the auto-picked best XI (`autoPickLineup` through `aiTeamInput`) scored by `computeTeamStrength`. Each club keeps its strongest season (ties to the earlier season), the clubs are ranked by that strength (ties to the earlier season, then the club id) and the top nineteen are the league (`superLeagueField`, `buildSuperClubs`); ranks 20 to 34 are the Continental Cup field (`buildSuperRestClubs`). The ranking is cached per universe and flag (`rankedClubs`, about 0.6 s over all seven leagues). It draws nothing and depends on no seed, so everyone faces the same nineteen; it depends on the leagues loaded, so a Super League game loads all of them (`leaguesFor` in the store, `openChallenge`, and Setup's league choice). With the data of 2026-10-25, classic on or off (no classic season makes the cut): Real Madrid 2017-18, Liverpool 2019-20, Manchester City 2023-24, Bayern Munich 2016-17, Paris Saint-Germain 2025-26, FC Barcelona 2018-19, Juventus 2017-18, Arsenal 2026-27, Inter 2008-09, Manchester United 2008-09, Chelsea 2008-09, Atlético Madrid 2018-19, Tottenham Hotspur 2017-18, Borussia Dortmund 2025-26, AC Milan 2023-24, Napoli 2022-23, Aston Villa 2025-26, Roma 2017-18 and Bayer Leverkusen 2024-25 (strengths 87.7 down to 83.1). The sides are named with their season, "Real Madrid 2017-18" (`AiClub.name`, `superSideName`, `sideNamer` for the screens), and keyed by club id as in any league.
- **The season.** `quickLeague(u, settings)` gives the quick season (`startRun`), the pundits (`oddsLeague`) and the cups their AI sides and handicap: in the Super League `buildSuperClubs` with no calibration (the Opposition setting does not apply) and an offset of `SUPER_LEAGUE_HANDICAP` (1 rating point off every line) when the rules carry `superEase`, which Setup writes beside `superLeague` from 2026-10-28; a game or link without it (everything before) keeps an offset of 0 and replays at full strength. The setup line says "in the Super League, a point below full strength" ("full strength" without the rule), otherwise `buildAiClubs` and `aiRatingOffset` exactly as before. Everything after that is the ordinary quick season on the game's `Rng`: 20 sides, 38 matchweeks (`quickSeasonGames` returns `SUPER_LEAGUE_GAMES`), the January window after matchweek 19 (listings from the wheel's leagues, which for the Super League are all seven), the bench, the manager, highlights and challenge replays. The points-record achievement and the record books are a real league's and are not offered; the summary names no real champion and no "real side".
- **After the league.** No domestic cup (`quickCupStatus` reports `SUPER_NO_CUP` and the Cup card is not drawn); after a top-seven finish the Continental Cup is played against `buildSuperRestClubs(u, classic, 15)` at full strength, the same rules and fork as any quick season's run.
- **Words.** `superTierLabel` (`tierName(tier, position, true)`): "Perfect Super League season", "Super League invincibles", "Super League champions", "Bottom three" for the last three (nobody goes down), the rest as in any league; `superTierBlurb` likewise. The pundits say "Win the Super League" and "Finish bottom three"; the header, the summary banner and the final table show the Super League mark (`SuperLeagueMark`, a crown in an accent chip, `QuickLeagueFlag`); the share text reads "Touchline XI, Super League" and the share card's subtitle "Super League"; the Profile's leaderboard row keeps `LeaderboardEntry.superLeague` to say the same.
- **Setup.** One more league card in step 3, League and season, after the real leagues (`data-testid="league-super"`, Quick Draft only, two or more leagues available), a `.chip.super` with the mark and its title (the hint "The 19 greatest sides in the data"); choosing it sets the wheel to every league and loads them, puts a "One season, the greatest sides" chip (`super-season`) where the season select sits, shows the help line under the cards (`league-help`), and hides the Opposition field in Advanced; the Quick Draft line above Spin (`quickSetupLine`) says "Super League" in place of the league count and never "Realistic", and the host chip of `setupChips` reads "Super League"; `SetupChoices.superLeague` makes `setupRules` write `superLeague: true` (no key otherwise). `describeRules` lists "Super League" for a challenge.
- **How hard.** The elite XI of `perfectChase.test.ts` taken across all seven leagues (overall 88.4) went 38-0 in 0 of 1,000 Super League seasons (never unbeaten, champions in 332, 20.7 wins on average; the pundits give 0% perfect, 43% for the title, 73 points), against 6 of 100 perfect seasons and 100 titles on the same seeds in the Premier League 2025-26. Reported, not tuned; `superLeague.test.ts` reruns a 30-seed sample.
- **A little easier** (2026-10-28, `DraftRules.superEase`, `SUPER_LEAGUE_HANDICAP = 1` in `src/engine/superLeague.ts`). Measured over 250 seeds (`slm-0` to `slm-249`, managers off, no January window, the data of 2026-10-25, engine 7) with three XIs: the assistant's own draft on the all-league wheel (team strength about 78.3), a strong XI of each 4-3-3 slot's 16th-best player (85.8, inside the field's 83.1 to 87.7) and the elite XI (89.0). At full strength: the assistant's XI 14.3 points (bottom three every time), the strong XI 50.5 (11th on average, champions 0.4%, top four 3.2%), the elite XI 77.2 (champions 61.6%, top four 91.2%), no 38-0 and no unbeaten season. With the point off: 18.3 (bottom three 97.2%), 57.0 (8.5th, top four 12.0%, champions 0%) and 83.2 (champions 84.0%, top four 97.6%, unbeaten 0.4%), still no 38-0: about six points more for the same strong or elite XI, four for the assistant's. Tried and set aside: 1.5 points (+5.4, +10.2, +8.3; the elite XI champions 88.4%) and 2 (+7.3, +13.9, +11.2; 94.8%), more than "a little". The same XIs in the Premier League 2025-26 (calibrated) take 57.6, 95.4 and 107.4 points (the elite XI perfect in 7 of 250), so the Super League stays far the harder. The rivals' handicap reaches the league, the pundits and the Super League's Continental Cup alike (`quickLeague`). A `superEase` link travels as version 2 (an older build says it comes from a newer version); the rule joins `NON_STANDARD_RULES` and `DAILY_RULES` (as `undefined`). `superLeague.test.ts` runs 30 seeds of the strong and elite XIs at both strengths (strong 53.4 to 59.4 points, elite 78.0 to 83.8, titles 17 to 27, no 38-0), and its non-Super pins are unchanged.
- **Nothing else moves.** No `ENGINE_VERSION` bump (it stayed `'4'`): a game without the rule takes the same draws in the same order. `superLeague.test.ts` pins three Premier League quick seasons with their January deal and cups, three European Nights seasons with classic seasons and the Continental Cup, their pundits' odds and the Daily of 2026-10-25 to hashes taken on the commit before the Super League (57f3453), and the whole existing suite passes unchanged.

### One-Nation Draft

`DraftRules.onlyNation` holds an ISO3 code as stored in `PlayerSeason.nationality` (`'BRA'`); `'UNK'`, the code for the 1,876 player-seasons whose nationality is not known, is never offered. The rule is one predicate, `nationAllows`, applied in `canUse`, `eligibleSlots` and `pick` (which throws "`{name} is not Brazil`"), so the wheel pool, the spin weighting, the assistant's auto-draft and the head-to-head usable list all inherit it: the wheel still never lands on a club-season without an eligible player, and in the squad list players of another nationality are dimmed with a "not Brazil" chip.

- **Setup.** The one-nation select (`nationDepth`, `src/engine/nations.ts`) lists the nations deep enough for the chosen shape in the chosen wheel leagues and era: for every position in the formation, at least 2 × its slot count in distinct humans who can cover it at `accomplished` fit (the draft's own predicate), and at least `squadSize + 6` distinct humans in all (11 + 6 for a quick draft). It is one pass over the loaded player-seasons (`nationCover`, about 160 ms for all seven leagues, memoised on wheel leagues, era and formation) and a cheap filter per squad size (`nationDepthFrom`), so the squad-size slider never rescans. With the 2026-10-24 data a 4-3-3 across all seven leagues offers 71 of the 161 known nations (68 at squad size 18, 65 at 22); England alone offers 26 (21 at 18). Côte d'Ivoire, with Premier League players in every era but no goalkeeper who can cover the position in the Premier League data (two across the seven leagues), is left out; Brazil counts 1,141 distinct players across the seven leagues and 107 in England. The option label reads `🇧🇷 Brazil · 1141 players`. The Daily ignores the rule.
- **Running dry.** The depth check covers year one; in a Dynasty the rule is copied into every summer draft, so bonus spins can exhaust a thin nation over the years. `canSpin(u, d)` is the pool-not-empty check: the store refuses to spin and toasts "The wheel has run dry for Brazil" instead of surfacing an engine error, `autoDraft` stops instead of throwing, and the Draft screen replaces the spin button with a notice. When the XI is full the draft may close short of the squad size (`finishDraft` accepts `picksMade >= 11` once `canSpin` is false); with slots still open the side cannot start and the notice says to go home and pick another nation.
- **Market and academy.** `MarketContext.onlyNation` travels from `settings.rules` into the January window, the Dynasty summer window, scouting trickle and every approach. `openWindow` samples only club-seasons that hold someone of the nation and takes only that nation's players from each, with a sampling budget of 60 tries per listing instead of 20 because repeats are frequent for a small nation; without the rule its random draws are unchanged. `enquire` answers an approach for anyone else, from the shortlist or the search box, with "The board wants a Brazil squad" and lists nothing. `buildYouthIntake` keeps only youngsters of the nation.
- **Record.** Winning the league with the rule on unlocks `one-nation-champions` ("International"), in both the season and the quick path. The share text gains the line `One nation: 🇧🇷 Brazil` before the seed, the summary shows the same chip, and the leaderboard entry stores `nation` and shows the flag beside the team name.

### Wheel twists

`DraftRules.twists` (on in `QUICK_RULES` and therefore in the Daily, off in `SEASON_RULES`; `startH2H` passes `twists: false`; a saved draft without the field reads as off) makes every third spin a **twist spin**: spins 3, 6 and 9 of a quick draft, so three of its eleven. `Twist = 'golden' | 'basement' | 'blind' | 'decade'`:

| Twist | Wheel | Where it is empty |
|---|---|---|
| 🥇 Golden | title-winning club-seasons only (`cs.champions`) | leagues without a table that season (`position` 0) |
| 🪣 Basement | the bottom three of their league that season: `position >= clubsThatSeason − 2`, with `clubsThatSeason` the row count of `u.tables[league][season]` (20 in England, Spain and Italy, 18 in the Bundesliga) | the same |
| 🙈 Blind | the plain wheel; the ratings of the players on offer and the slot chips are hidden until the pick (`ratingShown(rules, 'offer', { twist })`); once picked he shows his rating on the pitch and in the log, flipping in | never |
| 📅 Decade | club-seasons from the decade of the last pick (`Math.floor(season / 10)` of the last history row with a `pickedId`) | before the first pick |

Inside `spin()`: `d.twist` is cleared, and when `rules.twists && (d.spins + 1) % 3 === 0` the four twists are tried against `wheelPool(u, { ...d, twist })`, the ones with an empty wheel are dropped, and `rng.pick` chooses among the rest, one extra RNG draw on a twist spin and none on the others. Blind is always possible, so a twist spin always has a twist; in Ligue 1, the Eredivisie and the Primeira Liga, whose club-seasons all have `position` 0 and no `champions` flag, only blind and decade ever come up (tested over 30 seeds). `wheelPool` applies the twist as one more filter beside era, club, nation and league, so the spin weighting, the usefulness count, `canSpin`, the assistant and the reel all see the twisted pool: a golden spin runs the reel through the whole league that season with only the champion lit. The twist is recorded on the history row (`history[].twist`) and cleared by `pick()` and `respin()`; a re-spin on a twist spin logs the twist on the skipped row and lands on a plain spin number (spin 4 after re-spinning spin 3), which is left as it is rather than carried forward. "Auto-complete" clears a pending spin's twist with the spin it discards. `spin()` builds the rules pool (era, club, nation, league and the `canUse` check over every squad: 380 club-seasons in England, 2,526 across the seven leagues) once per spin and narrows it per twist, so the candidate check costs four cheap filters on a twist spin rather than four more passes over the squads; `wheelPool` returns exactly that narrowed pool.

The twist draw is part of the seed, so `ENGINE_VERSION` moved from `'1'` to `'2'` with this change: a seed drafted with twists on lands on the same first two clubs as before and diverges from the third spin. The Draft screen shows a `TwistCard` above the wheel (icon, name, one-line rule; a dashed "Twist spin next" card before a twist spin), the reel shows the twist's name beside the season drum ("Golden spin"), and the Setup screen has a toggle under Wheel rules (default on for Quick Draft, off for Season and Dynasty; the Daily is always on). The share text adds a line with the icons of the twists the picks went through, `Twists 🥇 🙈 📅`.

### The reel
