Nothing fires on a focused control, with a modal open or with a modifier, except on the Dugout's own tab strip (r10): digits and letters work there, and Space on the tab a click focused plays the week (`clickedTabAfter`: a key that moves the focus, Tab or an arrow, hands Space back to the tab) (`src/ui/dugoutKeys.ts`). P pauses the match. |

On the match day (`src/ui/match/highlights.ts`, `src/ui/match/assistant.ts`): **highlight levels** Full match, Extended and Key moments. Key watches each goal, penalty, red card, injury and chance of `shotXg` ≥ 0.25 with its three-minute build-up and the minute after; Extended adds yellows, substitutions and shots of 0.08 or more, and plays as Key once a side is three clear (`CRUISE_MARGIN`). The rest runs `QUIET_BOOST` (10) times faster through a `boost` on the clock store, never stored as the speed; goal holds and the stops are unchanged, and the segment is decided before it is shown. **The assistant's line** shows for four minutes from 15, 30, 60 and 80 (unless a moment takes the banner): an xG gap of 0.5 or more, else our lowest-rated starter at 6.2 or under (from 30), else their best at 7.5 or more, else the state of play, read at the checkpoint only. After a shout at 70 each stats flank shows "Since 70'": that side's xG and shots since the 70th minute.

### While you were away

Sim 5 and Sim to end (`store.simWeeks`) end on a digest of the run, a transient store field (`digest`, never saved) shown as a modal over the dugout. Before the mutation the store takes `digestSnapshot(game)`: plain numbers read from the saved state, never an Immer draft (the last matchweek played, `newsTotal`, the league position from `computeTable`, board confidence). After the loop `buildDigest(before, game)` (`src/game/logic/digest.ts`, pure and RNG-free) collects:

- the results: every league fixture of yours with `matchweek` in (from, to], then every cup and continental tie of yours with a result and `afterMatchweek` in that range, labelled by round, a level tie with its penalty score; sorted by matchweek with the league match before the midweek tie. League results are W, D or L; a knockout tie is W when you went through and L when you went out;
- the position before and after, the league points of those weeks only, and confidence before and after;
- the new inbox items, `news.slice(0, newsTotal − before.newsTotal)`, flagged `truncated` when more were posted than the 120-item feed still holds, split by kind into injuries, suspensions, transfers and development (the training reports);
- the decisions: when the assistant resolves a pending event during a managed week (it takes the second option, as before) `playMatchweek` first posts an `event` item titled `Assistant decided: <event title>` with the chosen option's label as its body, so the choice is on record in the Inbox; the digest lists each one with the outcome text from the event's own item, the nearest event item of the same title above the record and short of that week's result (a training setback may sit between them). The same call with the same answer and outcome twice in one run (the weekly press question) is one line with its count, `Press conference (2 times): One game at a time`.

The modal is titled `Matchweeks 12 to 16` (`Matchweek 16` for a single week). It shows a strip of result chips (opponent badge, score, H or A, dashed for a cup or Europe tie with the shoot-out score when it went to penalties, green for W and red for L), `3W 2D 0L · 9th to 6th, 11 points from 5 league matches` (`Still 6th` when unchanged, and `6th in the table` for a run from pre-season, when the table before it is only an order of clubs on no points), two confidence bars (before, then after), the groups `Decided for you`, Injuries, Suspensions, Transfers and Development at most `DIGEST_GROUP_CAP` (8) lines each with `and N more in the Inbox`, a line when the run was quiet (three wordings), a one-line read of the stretch at the top (r13, `digestMood` in `src/ui/seasonDrama.ts`: every league match won, none lost over three or more, none won, a climb or a slide of three places or more (not from pre-season), then two points a game or more, under one, or mixed; three wordings each in the `app` catalogue, picked by `digestWording` from a hash of the game and the weeks; none for a single league match), the Inbox's unread count, read live from the game exactly as the tab badge counts it (this run's items plus any left unread before it; `Nothing unread` when the run was simulated with the Inbox open, which marks the batch read as it lands) and a `To the dugout` button (Escape closes it too). Starting or resuming another game clears it. There is no digest when nothing was played, or when the run ended the season: the season summary covers that. A sacking ends the season too, so a run that ends in the sack goes to the summary without a digest.

The digest ends on where the run leaves the manager (r10, `digestAhead` in `src/game/logic/digestAhead.ts`, pure, display only, read from the game after the run): the next matchweek and its league opponent (or a free weekend), then every squad man who misses it, injured (matchweeks out) or banned (matches), longest out first, at most `AHEAD_CAP` (5) named and the rest counted, or "The whole squad is available".

### Storylines (display only)

A Season's and a Dynasty's sense of story, read from what the game already holds: no decision, no draw, nothing stored. The facts are `src/game/logic/storylines.ts` (pure, no RNG, no clock, never imported by a simulation, store or online module) and the words `src/ui/storyText.ts` (through `format.ts`; where a line has several wordings, one is picked by a hash of the game id, the year and the matchweek through `flavour.ts`, so a week always reads the same). A line's wordings are the catalogue's `stem_0`, `stem_1`… (`i18n/en/story.ts`), counted at run time by `variantsOf(stem)`, so a new wording is one new message; since content round 2 (r13) nearly every line has two to five (the form line seven ways for a side on a run, a perfect last five its own, `LONG_RUN` 8 wins its own), and `src/ui/__tests__/contentR13.test.ts` reaches every wording of every stem through the function a screen calls. `contentDisplayOnly.test.ts` plays a seeded Season and a Dynasty into its summer with every storyline, preview and review read and finds them byte-identical to the same games never read.

- **Match preview** (the Next match tab's first box, `preview` in the Boxes control, `MatchPreviewPanel`): the next league match (`nextLeagueFixture`) and the midweek tie when there is one (`nextTieFixture`), each with the two sides' last five league results as W/D/L chips and a line on them (`formLine`: one side's four wins in five, three defeats in five, an unbeaten run, else "little to choose"); the meetings already played this season in any competition (`meetingsWith`, the shoot-out score included); what is at stake (`leagueStake` from the table from matchweek 3: a title decider when both sides are in the top two and three, a title race when within 6 points of the top, a relegation six-pointer when both are in the bottom six, survival in the bottom six against anyone, a top-four chase from 4th to 7th within 4 points, a leapfrog when the opponent is within 3 points above; a tie's round: final, semi-final or "lose and you are out"); their player to watch (`theirWatch`: their fit top scorer with two goals or more, else their best man) and yours (`ourWatch`: the starter averaging 7.2 or more over his last five ratings, else the top scorer, else the best starter); a derby (`derbyOf`: a named derby from `DERBIES`, 43 real pairs keyed by the two club ids in order, common names only and no marks; else the host club's or the opponent's `rivals`; else a shared `city`; never in the Continental Cup); and the old boys (`oldBoysOf`: squad men whose player-season was at the opponent, never the club the user replaced).
- **Running storylines** (`storylines`, strongest first by `STORY_WEIGHT`): a title race (in the top three from matchweek max(10, 40 % of the season), within `RACE_GAP` 6 points of the other end of the top, with the week before's gap for "closing in" or "pulling clear"); a relegation fight (in the bottom six from max(8, 30 %), within 6 points of the line either side, with the trend); the board's patience (confidence under `PRESSURE_CONFIDENCE` 30 from matchweek 6, "the job is on the line" with job security on); the run-in (`RUN_IN_GAMES` 6 or fewer league games left: the gap to the top, the drop or the board's target); milestones (50, 100 ... 300 appearances or goals for the club, a Dynasty's `careerStats` plus this season: one game from an appearance milestone, within two goals of a goal milestone, or crossed in the week just played, read from the stored league and cup results of that week); a former club's visit (the first old boy of the next league match or midweek tie); the Golden Boot race (from max(8, 25 %): your top scorer with 5 or more, within 3 of the league's leader over every club's season statistics, with the leader or the nearest rival); and the run you are on (`currentRuns`: 4 straight wins, 6 unbeaten, 3 straight defeats or 5 without a win, the unbeaten and winless runs only when not told as the stronger one). The Inbox shows them in a Storylines box above the feed (All only; not counted as unread), and the Dugout's fixture line shows the strongest as a chip (`story-chip`) with its sentence as the tooltip.
- **A Dynasty summer** (`summerReview`, from the summer wheel to the first league game): the season just finished from `game.history` against the one before: a headline (`treble`, `perfect`, `double`, `invincible`, `champions`, `relegated`, `cup`, `europe` for a top-four finish, `climb` or `slide` for three places or more in the same league, else `steady`) on the Dugout's pre-season box, and a "story of last season" card at the top of the Inbox: the finish and record, goals, the cups (the Continental Cup's furthest round), the top scorer and player of the season, the year before, the titles so far and the all-time top scorer from the legends. `seasonStory` needs the season's fixtures, which the summer has already dropped, so the card is told from the stored record instead.

## 11. The transfer market

`src/engine/market.ts` and `src/game/logic/marketOps.ts`.

Scouting knowledge and masked ranges, the recruitment focus, scout reports, release clauses, finding a buyer and sell-on shares are in section 10, Recruitment; with none of them in use the market below runs exactly as written.

**Windows.** The January window opens after matchweek 19 with 36 listings and 7 market days at price level 1.05. Two days pass automatically with each matchweek you play, and you can advance a day at a time from the Market tab; left alone it shuts after matchweek 23. Dynasty summers open a 40-listing, 10-day window at price level 1.0 that stays open through pre-season and closes at kick-off. Listings respect your era filters.

**Listings** are sampled from club-seasons across the eras, one to three players each, biased to the top of each squad, never a human you already own, and in a one-nation game (section 5) only players of that nation from club-seasons that hold one. Suitors: 1 to 3 for an 85+, 0 to 2 for a 78+, else 0 to 1.

**Asking price** starts from value × price level, then:

| Rule | Multiplier | Tag |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Overall 88+ | ×1.35 | World class premium |
| Overall 84 to 87 | ×1.15 | Star premium |
| Age ≤ 22 and overall ≥ 75 | ×1.15 | High potential |
| Age 33+ | ×0.70 | Veteran discount |
| Won the league that season | ×1.08 | Title winner |
| Last five ratings average ≥ 7.6 (own players) | ×1.15 | In form |
| Average ≤ 6.3 over 3+ games | ×0.85 | Out of form |
| Injured 6+ weeks | ×0.60 | Long-term injury |
| Injured | ×0.90 | Injured |

Prices round to the nearest £1M above 20, £0.5M above 5, else £0.1M.

**Each market day:**

- A listing with suitors has a 35 per cent chance of rising by `4% × suitors` (Bidding war).
- An 80+ player gains a suitor 8 per cent of the time.
- With two or more suitors and no bid from you, 12 per cent chance he signs for a league rival (Gone).
- On the last day, unwanted players (no suitors) drop 15 per cent 60 per cent of the time (Deadline discount).
- 60 per cent chance of one to three new listings.
- For each of your players, `desirability = (overall − 72) / 20 + 0.2 if aged ≤ 25 + 0.3 if morale < 45`; if positive, a `5% × desirability` chance of an offer at 75 to 110 per cent of his asking price from a league club. Accepting banks the fee and releases him; other open offers for him are withdrawn.

**AI signings are real.** When a listing goes to a league rival (two or more suitors, 12 per cent a day; one suitor, 4 per cent), the player-season joins that club's squad and state for the rest of the season, as does any player you sell to them.

**Loans.** Fringe players and youngsters (`loanable`: age ≤ 23 and overall ≤ 83, or overall ≤ 76) are listed with a loan fee of 12 per cent of asking (minimum £0.2M) about half the time. A loan signing pays the fee and full wages, carries `contract.loan`, and goes back to his club in the Dynasty summer.

**Part-exchange.** A bid may include one of your players. The selling club takes him only if he is within eight rating points of the man they lose, under 34 and not injured for four weeks or more, and credits 80 per cent of his asking price (60 per cent past thirty) towards theirs. The bid is judged on cash plus credit.

**Approaches.** Any player in the loaded database can be approached from the market tab (or the shortlist). The club quotes asking × premium by his standing in their squad: ×1.6 for a top-three player, ×1.4 top eight, ×1.25 top fourteen, else ×1.1, tagged Unsolicited approach; one of their top three rated 86+ is refused outright 30 per cent of the time (50 if they won the league). Approached players never sign elsewhere, and fringe ones may be loaned.

**Bidding.** You must have the fee in the budget and room under the wage cap, and fewer than 23 players. Contract length is your choice (one to four years; two at most for a 33-year-old), and so is the squad status he signs on, which sets his wage demand and which he may refuse (section 10, Squad status and playing time). At 97 per cent of asking or more: accepted. Between 85 and 97: accepted with probability `(ratio − 0.85) / 0.12 × 0.7` (+0.15 if nobody else is interested), otherwise a counter at 94 to 100 per cent of asking. Below 85: laughed out, asking +5 per cent, tagged Annoyed. Signings join the bench at familiarity 20, sharpness 65 on a three-year deal (one year at 32+). Signing for under 70 per cent of the opening price unlocks Dealmaker.

## 12. Awards, tiers, achievements and the share text

**Season tiers** (`seasonTier`): PERFECT (every game won), INVINCIBLE (first, unbeaten), CHAMPIONS (first), TITLE_RACE (second), EUROPEAN (3rd to 6th), MID_TABLE (7th to 15th), RELEGATION_BATTLE (16th to 17th), RELEGATED (18th to 20th, or sacked). The ids are saved (the game, history, challenge claims, daily results) and never change; the words shown are short plain outcomes in sentence case (`TIER_LABEL`, `tierLabel(tier, position)`, `tierName` in `format.ts`): Perfect season, Invincibles, Champions, Title race, Top 4 (EUROPEAN in 3rd or 4th) or Top 6 (5th or 6th, and wherever the position is not known, as in a challenge claim), Mid-table, Relegation battle, Relegated. Until 2026-10-25 EUROPEAN read "EUROPEAN NIGHTS" and PERFECT "38-0: THE PERFECT SEASON", and on `season-show` briefly TOP-FOUR FINISH and TOP-SIX FINISH. The summary puts one short line under the headline (`tierBlurb(tier, position, teams, sacked)`, at most about 40 characters), for example "4th of 20, a top-four side.", "Every game won." or "Sacked at 12th of 20."

**Awards** (`computeAwards`, Season and Dynasty): Player of the Season (best average rating with 15+ appearances), Golden Boot (most goals), Playmaker (most assists), Golden Glove (goalkeeper with most clean sheets; a clean sheet needs 60 minutes), Young Player (23 or under, 15+ apps), and a Team of the Season laid out in a 4-3-3 by average rating. Quick modes report the top scorer and the best average over 15+ apps.

**Achievements** (35, `ACHIEVEMENTS` in `src/engine/awards.ts`, stored with the unlock date in the profile):

| Id | Title | Condition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| perfect | 38-0 | Win every league match |
| invincible | Invincibles | Unbeaten champions |
| champions | Champions | Win the league |
| centurions | Centurions | 100 points |
| double | The Double | League and cup in one season |
| cup | Cup Winners | Lift the cup |
| continental | Kings of the Continent | Win the Continental Cup |
| treble | The Treble | League, cup and Continental Cup in one season |
| boot30 | Thirty Goals | A player scores 30 |
| glove20 | Wall | 20 clean sheets |
| hard-champions | Blindfolded | Win the league after a hard draft (`difficultyOf` is hard) |
| one-club-champions | Loyalty | Win the league with a one-club draft |
| one-nation-champions | International | Win the league with a one-nation draft |
| quick-perfect | Quick 38-0 | Perfect season in a quick draft |
| daily-streak-7 | Regular | Seven daily challenges running |
| dynasty-3 | Dynasty | Three titles in a row |
| survivor | Survivor | Keep the job after confidence fell under 20 |
| dealmaker | Dealmaker | Sign a player under 70% of his opening price |
| record-points | Record Books | Set an outright league points record on Realistic over a full season |
| moneyball | Bargain Hunters | Win the league on a salary cap of £150M or less for an XI (section 5) |
| chain-champions | Dressing Room | Win the league with an unbroken teammate chain (section 5) |
| final-whistle | Final Whistle | Win a one-off final (section 17) |
| spot-kick-kings | Spot-Kick Kings | Win a shoot-out in the arena |
| gauntlet-5 | Five Rungs | Climb five rungs of the Gauntlet |
| gauntlet-10 | Last One Standing | Climb ten rungs of the Gauntlet |
| gauntlet-15 | Gauntlet Complete | Climb all fifteen rungs of the Gauntlet |
| giant-killer | Giant Killer | Beat a side with a real team strength of 85+ in the arena on Realistic |
| cup-night | Cup Night | Lift the arena's Cup |
| cup-32 | The Long Road | Win a 32-club Cup |
| giant-slayers | Giant Slayers | Win the Cup against the giants field |
| clean-run | Clean Run | Win the Cup without conceding a goal |
| arena-regular | Arena Regular | Play the Daily Arena seven days running |
| weekly-streak-4 | Month of Sundays | Play the Weekly Challenge four weeks running |
| great-escape | The Great Escape | Keep a relegated side up in a run-in (section 10) |
| run-in-title | Late Show | Win the title from matchweek 28 in a run-in |

**Share text** (`shareText`), copied to the clipboard from the summary screen:

```
TouchLine · Quick Draft
Touchline XI as Burnley, 2025-26
CHAMPIONS
29W 6D 3L · 93 pts · 1st
🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟩🟨🟨🟨🟨🟨🟨🟥🟥🟥
⚽ E. Haaland 27
⭐ K. De Bruyne
Seed K7Q2-MX9A
```

Line two names the host club and season; line three is the tier plus "+ CUP" where it applies; the block row is one square per win, draw and defeat; a line such as `Hard · Position First · Prime ratings` names the 38-0 options that differ from the mode's defaults (section 5); a Quick Draft, Daily or Weekly then prints the draft's grade from the recap, `Draft grade B` (`draftGradeLine`, also an accent line on the share card); the daily variant labels the mode "Daily 2026-10-24" and omits the seed line; Dynasty labels it "Dynasty Y2".

**The share text's record** reads `31-4-3 · 97 pts · 1st` (won, drawn, lost; it was `31W 4D 3L`), the tier line adds ` + Cup` and ` + Europe`, and a quick season (Quick Draft or the Daily) names the team alone, `Touchline XI, 2025-26`, never "as" the club whose place it takes (`hostClubName` empty); a managed season keeps `Touchline XI as Burnley, 2025-26`. A quick season short of perfect adds its runs when they say something (`runsLine`: `Perfect run 12-0 · unbeaten for the first 15`, `Perfect run ended in game 2`, `Unbeaten for the first 9 games`; nothing for a first game not won with no unbeaten run after it, and an unbeaten season leaves the unbeaten part out).

`extraLines` run in this order: the salary cap (`💰 £178M of a £180M cap · 0.52 pts per £M`, section 5), the teammate chain (`🔗 Teammate chain · 11 links`, section 5), one nation, twists, the record-books line, the Season Story moment, the verdict against the real club, the challenge verdict. For example `🏆 Title sealed at MW 34 with 4 to play` then `📊 The real Burnley finished 19th on 22 · you 1st on 93` before the seed line.

**Share card** (`renderShareCard`, `src/ui/shareCard.ts`, 1080 by 1350). Optional fields, all additive, so a card without them draws exactly as before: `strip` (one 22 px cell per game under the record, up to 46, 4 px gaps), `moment` (one 30 px line under the strip), `banner` (`{ text, colour? }` in place of the tier label, for the arena modes) and `lines` (up to six lines in place of the W/D/L row and the points line, for knockout ties or a ladder). The pitch moves down and shortens by whatever sits above it. The summary passes the strip and the moment, and after a teammate-chain draft two `lines`: the record (`29W 6D 3L · 93 points · finished 1st`) and the chain of club codes.

**Share, round 8** (`src/game/logic/shareInvite.ts`, display only, never imported by the verifier; the card's and the share text's words live there, untranslated). Three more optional card fields: `mode` (the mode in a pill at the top), `beatMe` (an `Invite` from `inviteFor(kind, facts)`: a title, a line and where to play, for ten kinds from `inviteKindOf(game)`: "Beat my draft · Same spins, same wheel. Beat 93 points.", "Beat today's Daily", "Beat this week's draft", "Can you top this?" for Season and Dynasty, "Beat my final", "Beat my cup run", "Beat my climb" with the rung to pass, "Take the same job" with the place to finish above, "Draft against a friend"; with a short `#s=` code the address reads `touchline.quest · code K7Q2MX9A`) and `versus` (`versusOf`: you against the challenger after a ghost draft, decided as `verdictLine` decides it, points then fewer defeats). The share text goes through `tidyShareText` (the form blocks 🟩🟨🟥⬛⬜ and a result line's own icon stay; flags, which Windows prints as two letters, go; a ⚽ or ⭐ used as a label becomes a word; any other picture goes) and `shareWithInvite` adds the invitation line, your challenge link once made. Home's reasons to come back come from the same module: `dailyNudge` (played today, a streak that ends tonight, or fresh), `personalBests` (the best record, the longest perfect run, rungs climbed, the Daily Arena and Weekly streaks) and `nextBadge` (one already started first, the most of the way there); the quick summary's `oneMore` says how the season stands against your best record.

### In the record books

`src/engine/records.ts` is pure lookups over the real league tables already shipped in `u.tables` (the season tables of each pack plus England's historic 1992-93 to 2007-08 tables: the three 22-club seasons of 42 games until 1994-95 form their own peer group; points are net of any deduction, and `realChampion` names nobody for a season in `u.voidTitles`); nothing in it touches the RNG.

- **Complete seasons only.** `historicRows(u, leagueId)` returns every row of every complete real season, oldest first. `LeagueSeasonRow` carries no `played`, so a row's matches are `won + drawn + lost`; a season counts when every row adds up to `2 × (clubs − 1)`, which skips the season in progress (`u.currentSeason`, 2026-27 with four to seven matches played); seasons that ship without a table (the Bundesliga's 2008-09 and 2009-10, La Liga before 2012-13, Serie A before 2013-14) never reach `u.tables` (`buildLeague` drops empty tables), and an empty table would be skipped too. With the 2026-10-24 data that is 26 Premier League seasons (520 club-seasons of 38 matches), 14 La Liga, 13 Serie A and 16 Bundesliga seasons (288 club-seasons of 34 matches); Ligue 1, the Eredivisie and the Primeira Liga ship without tables and return nothing.
- **Same length only.** `benchmarkSeason(u, leagueId, row)` compares the user's season with the historic rows of the same `played`, so a 34-game Bundesliga campaign is never measured against a 38-game one and a sacked or otherwise unfinished season matches nothing. Five metrics: points, wins and goals scored (more is better), goals conceded and defeats (fewer is better). Each reports `rank` (1 + the historic rows strictly better), `of` (historic rows compared), `equal` (rows level with it) and `better` (the top three ahead of it, best first, ties broken by season then club id). `records` lists the metrics where the season stands alone at the top (`rank === 1` and `equal === 0`): Manchester City's real 2017-18 ranks first on points but ties itself, so it is not an outright record, while 101 points is. `seasons` is the number of distinct seasons compared and a league has a record book only from `RECORD_BOOK_MIN_SEASONS = 10` of them (`hasRecordBook`).
- **Real champions.** `realChampion(u, leagueId, season)` is the position-one row of a complete table, null for a missing table or the season in progress.
- **Summary panel.** "In the record books" sits under the tier banner when the league has a record book and the season was not cut short: one row per metric with the figure, its all-time standing ("5th best of 521", "Joint best", or a gold "League record" chip with a scroll icon), the three seasons ahead of it (hidden on a phone), and, behind the title's info button, how many seasons it is measured against and the real champions of the season you played in. Under Calibrated difficulty one line says these are benchmarks, not records, with the reason (the AI clubs were handicapped, section 13) behind an info button. It is distinct from the Stats tab's within-season "Season records" (streaks, biggest win; `seasonRecords` in `src/game/logic/seasonRecords.ts`, our score first: `9-0 at Nott'm Forest`).
- **Share text.** `recordShareLine` adds `📜 New Premier League points record: 101`, `📜 Joint-best points total in Premier League history` or `📜 5th-best points total in Premier League history` to `extraLines` for a top-ten points total; a mid-table season adds nothing.
- **Achievement.** `record-points` ("Record Books") unlocks in both `finishSeason` and `runQuickSeason` through `earnsPointsRecord` (`src/game/logic/records.ts`): Realistic difficulty, not sacked, `won + drawn + lost` equal to the season's matchweeks, and an outright points record in a league with a record book. Calibrated hundred-point seasons stay benchmarks.
- **Dynasty history** gains a "Real champions" column from each `SeasonRecord`'s `leagueId` and `leagueSeason`, a dash where no complete table exists.

### Season, game by game

`src/game/logic/gameByGame.ts`. The quick season has always played all 380 fixtures with the real match engine; `recordUserGames` now keeps the user's 38 (34 in an 18-club league) as `QuickResult.results`, one `QuickGameResult` per matchweek in the order they were played: `matchweek`, `opponentId`, `home`, `gf`, `ga`, the user's `scorers` (every `goal`, `penalty` or `own_goal` event on the user's side, ordered by minute, with the player-season id and name; an opponent's own goal is credited as "OG" with no id) and `position`, the user's league place after that matchweek.

- **Pure bookkeeping.** It runs after the fixture loop over fixtures that already carry their results, so it draws no random numbers: the final table, the RNG state and the top scorer of seed `game-by-game` (Burnley 2025-26, 4-3-3, auto-drafted) are pinned in a test against the values the engine produced before the recording existed (Arsenal 80 points, the user second on 79 with 24W 7D 7L and 87-44). The position is computed once per matchweek, not per fixture: 38 calls to `computeTable` over at most 380 fixtures.
- **The field is optional** and read with `?? []`, so a save or a leaderboard entry written before it existed still opens; nothing about the replay is stored.
- **The panel.** "Season, game by game" sits directly under the tier banner on the summary of a quick draft or the Daily, newest game first by default with a "Newest / Oldest" switch (the choice is a per-viewer convenience in `localStorage` key `touchline-gbg-order`, inside try/catch; `gamesInOrder`, pure and tested). Each row is tinted by the result (green win, amber draw, red defeat) and reads: a W, D or L badge, the opponent's badge and name with an H or A tag, the score as `gf-ga`, "MW n · 4th" (the matchweek and the position after it) and, on a second line, the scorers as "⚽ Haaland 12', 67' · OG 80'" (minutes grouped per scorer in order of his first goal, initials dropped). The row is a grid of 28 / 22 / 1fr / auto / auto px (plus 30 px for the Highlights button) with the scorers under it, so it holds at 390 px wide. The head-to-head lists stay in matchweek order.
- **Folded.** `GameByGame` takes `fold` to show only the first games with a "Show all 38 games" button (`shownGames` in `src/ui/screenText.ts`); while the replay runs every revealed game shows, and "Show fewer" folds it again. The quick summary does not fold its list: on a desktop it has its own box and scrolls inside it. The head-to-head lists are not folded (they scroll inside their panel).
- **The replay** (`useSeasonReplay`) is UI state only. Everything is shown on arrival; "Play the season" empties the list and reveals one game every `REPLAY_STEP_MS = 220` ms (8.4 s for 38 games), the strip and the position update as games land, the banner shows "Season in play…" with the running strip in place of the tier and record, and the tier pops in after the last game. When the banner shows its result the W, D and L and the points count up from zero over 0.4 s, and a title earns a gold flare behind the tier name (the perfect season adds a burst of confetti), the first time that result is shown in a visit and again after every replay; all of it is UI only and off under reduced motion. "Skip" shows the rest at once; `prefers-reduced-motion` offers no replay button and animates nothing. The head-to-head result shows both managers' lists (`H2HResult.results`, the two derbies against the other manager included) without the replay.
- **Share text.** `bestWinLine` adds `Best win: 5-0 v Chelsea (MW 23)` (or `3-0 at Chelsea`) to `extraLines`: the widest margin, then the most goals scored, then the earliest matchweek; a season without a win adds nothing. Since Season Story the share text carries the moment line too, whose last fallback is the same best win (`💥 Best: 5-0 v Chelsea (MW 23)`), so the summary leaves `bestWinLine` out when a story is told.

### Season Story

The moments of a finished league season, from results that already exist: no random number is drawn, so every pin and the 20 quick-season RNG states in `src/game/__tests__/story.test.ts` (computed before the story existed) hold.

- **When it was decided** (`clinchMatchweeks` in `src/engine/story.ts`). After each matchweek `m`, a club's ceiling is its points plus 3 for each fixture it still has after `m` (fixtures, not matchweeks, so a bye week is no game). The title is sealed (`clinchedAt`) when the user's points beat every other club's ceiling; relegation is certain (`doomedAt`) when at least `n − 3` clubs already have more points than the user's ceiling. Once every fixture is played the final table decides, so a title won on goal difference is sealed on the last day and `clinchedAt` is set exactly when the user finishes first (`doomedAt` exactly when in the bottom three). A season cut short (a sacking) is read only up to the last fully played matchweek. One `computeTable` per matchweek.
- **The story** (`seasonStory` in `src/game/logic/story.ts`): the strip (W, D or L per game in order), the longest winning and unbeaten runs with their matchweek spans (the first one on a tie), clean sheets, games without a goal, the biggest win (`bestWin`) and the heaviest defeat (widest margin, then most conceded, then earliest), the first game that dropped points after a winning start, and one moment line by priority:
  1. Sacked: `🪑 Sacked after MW 21`.
  2. PERFECT: `💎 38 from 38 · closest call 1-0 v Spurs (MW 31)` (smallest margin, then fewest scored, then the latest).
  3. INVINCIBLE: `🛡️ Unbeaten · title sealed at MW 33`, or `… won on the last day`.
  4. CHAMPIONS: `🏆 Title sealed at MW 34 with 4 to play` (the user's games left), or `🏁 Won it on the last day` (sealed in the final matchweek, or with no game left).
  5. RELEGATED: `⬇️ Down at MW 35`, or `⬇️ Relegated on the last day`.
  6. A winning start of `STORY_START_MIN = 8` or more that ended: `💔 12-0 until MW 13: 1-1 v Everton`.
  7. A winning run of `STORY_RUN_MIN = 6` or more: `📈 9-match winning run (MW 14-22)`.
  8. The best win: `💥 Best: 5-0 v Chelsea (MW 23)`; without a win, the longest unbeaten run (`🧱 Unbeaten in 3 (MW 3-5), never a win`) or `🥶 38 played, 38 lost`.
- **Where it is kept.** Nowhere new. Since main merged in (2026-10-25) a quick season stores nothing for the story, so its result is byte for byte main's (`noMove.test.ts`): `storyForGame` works the clinch weeks out of `QuickResult.rounds` (`roundsFixtures`, every club's results by matchweek) and the moment from `results`; a save from the casual-modes branch that stored `clinchedAt`, `doomedAt` and `moment` is read as it was. Season and Dynasty keep nothing new either: `storyForGame` retells the finished season from `season.fixtures` through `recordUserGames` and `clinchMatchweeks`. A quick save with neither (written before `rounds`) has no clinch week, so its champion line reads `🏆 Champions · top from MW 20` (the first matchweek from which the user stayed top) and a relegated one `⬇️ In the bottom three from MW 30`; a quick save without `results` has no story.
- **Measured** (200 auto-drafted quick seasons, seeds `story-measure-0` to `-199`, as Burnley 2025-26, England, 4-3-3, Calibrated, engine `'4'`, the current packs): 40 titles (20%), sealed between MW 32 and 38, median MW 37, 12 of them on the last day; no relegation; moments 124 best win, 36 winning run, 28 title sealed with games to play, 12 last-day title, none a winning start that ended (an auto-drafted XI rarely opens with eight straight wins).
- **Verdict against the real club** (`realSeasonRow`, `realityLine`): when the host club-season is complete and the league has its table, `📊 The real Burnley finished 19th on 22 · you 1st on 93`, in every mode.
- **Summary.** The moment sits under the W/D/L record in the tier banner; a "Season story" panel under the banner shows the moment, the strip (one cell per game, `.blocks`), the title or relegation week, the runs, the winning start, clean sheets, blanks and the best and worst results. A quick season keeps its game-by-game list and replay (both wait for the replay to finish); Season and Dynasty get the same list, compact, from the fixtures. The Profile page shows `LeaderboardEntry.moment` as a muted line under the team in Best seasons (`recordResult` files it).
- **The pundit's verdict** (`src/game/logic/pundit.ts`, display only): under the moment, one or two short sentences on the result of a Quick Draft, Daily, Weekly, Season or Dynasty year (not a run-in or the arena): sacked, a Treble, perfect, unbeaten, a Double, champions, the title race, Europe, mid-table, survival or the drop (`VERDICT_LINES`, 66 lines since content round 2), then two times in three a word on a number that stands out (a hundred points or more, 2.4 goals a game or more, 0.6 conceded or fewer, 1.7 conceded in the top half, a third of games drawn; `STAT_LINES`, 22) or, from a Dynasty's second season, its run (ten seasons, at least two titles and one every other year, or a project taking shape; `DYNASTY_LINES`, 11), all picked by a hash of the season's id (`${game.id}-${game.year}`). Season Story's own lines are untouched.

**Profile** (local only): best 50 seasons by points then fewest defeats, daily streak and per-day results, games started, seasons simulated, manager name, theme (Auto, which follows the device's `prefers-color-scheme` live, Light or Dark; the top bar's sun and moon switch stores Light or Dark), and the Trophy Room's cabinet (below).

### Cabinet and Trophy Room

- **The cabinet** (`Profile.cabinet?: Cabinet`, `src/game/types.ts`; logic in `src/game/logic/cabinet.ts`, pure, no RNG). It holds the five season trophy counts (league, cup, continental, doubles, trebles), perfect seasons, unbeaten champions (perfect ones included), 100-point seasons, `hostsTitled` (each club whose place you took when you won a league, once), `nationsTitled` (each nation of a one-nation draft that won a league, once), results per mode (`byMode`), dailies, the head-to-head seasons played, and shelves for the casual modes (weeklies, arena dailies, Gauntlet runs and best, arena cups by size, giant kills, run-ins played and met), each credited by its mode (sections 3, 10 and 17).
- **Crediting.** `recordResult` calls `creditGame(cabinet, game, { newDaily })` once per result, for every mode: `alreadyCredited` skips a result whose id is on the leaderboard or in `Cabinet.recent` (the last 20 ids credited), so a result that missed a full top 50 is not credited a second time. A title is first place on a season that was not sacked (`isTitle`: a sacked season is filed RELEGATED whatever the table said, so it wins nothing in the league, unlike the old counter); the cup and the Continental Cup come from the last history row; the host is that row's club (a dynasty can move). `countsAsLeagueSeason(mode)` is exhaustive over `Mode`: a one-tie, nine-game or projected trophy (the arena, the run-in) must answer no and credit its own shelf, so it never counts as a league title or a cup. `h2hFinish` credits `h2h.played` once per head-to-head (the first time it is played, after the season has run), the only record a hot-seat game leaves. `newDaily` is set when the result filed its day for the first time, so `dailies` counts days. `profile.trophies` is still written, as the cabinet's five counts (`trophiesOf`), so an older build reading the save sees the same numbers.
- **Migration.** The persist `merge` gives a profile without a cabinet `rebuildCabinet(profile)`: the five trophy counts copied exactly, perfect, invincible, centurions, `hostsTitled`, `nationsTitled` and `byMode` counted from the 50-entry leaderboard, `dailies` from the `dailyResults` keys, and `rebuilt: true` when there was a leaderboard to count, which shows "counted from your 50 best seasons before the Trophy Room" on the Profile page. A cabinet that is present is filled out by `normaliseCabinet` and caught up with `profile.trophies` (`reconcileTrophies`: each of the five counts takes the larger), since a build from before the Trophy Room keeps the cabinet but advances only `trophies`.
- **The honours board** (`Cabinet.honours?: HonourEntry[]`, optional, absent on cabinets before it; `normaliseCabinet` fills `[]` and drops malformed rows): every Treble and Double season, newest first, at most `HONOURS_CAP = 30`, one entry per season (`resultId`) with its best honour, so a Double that the second cup turns into a Treble replaces its own entry and keeps its date (`fileHonour`, which never downgrades). `creditGame` files a Season or Dynasty season's honour when its result is credited (`honourEntry`: kind, the cups, team name, mode, league, season, `superLeague`, the date from `recordResult`); a Quick Draft's cups file theirs as they are won (`creditCupRun` in `playCup`). The Profile page's Trophy cabinet panel lists them under "Honours board" (a crown for a Treble, a trophy for a Double, the team, league flag and season, mode and date).
- **Leagues won in** are derived, not stored (`leaguesWon`): each titled host's league from the loaded clubs, or from every pack's club list (`fetchClubLeagues`, the small `meta.json` files only, fetched by the Profile page when a host is not loaded), plus `LeaderboardEntry.leagueId`, which entries carry from now on.
- **Badges** (`BADGES`, `src/engine/badges.ts`, reading a structural `BadgeCabinet`): `badgeTier` gives bronze, silver or gold, `badgeProgress` "3 of 5 for silver" (or "Gold"), and a badge at zero shows greyed.
- **Milestones** (`MILESTONE_BADGES`, `src/game/logic/milestoneBadges.ts`, display only, the engine's badges unchanged): nineteen more badges read at render from what the cabinet and profile already keep, nothing new stored. Tiered ones show a thin bar to the next tier; a stamp has one threshold and is gold once earned ("Not yet" before). Those marked "board" count the 50-entry leaderboard, so a long career may hold more (the tile's tooltip says so). Invincibles (unbeaten titles, 1 / 3 / 10), Treble Winners (1 / 3 / 10), Double Act (1 / 5 / 15), Continental Kings (1 / 5 / 15), Winning Machine (the best run of wins from the first game, `quickBests.longestPerfectRun`, 10 / 20 / 38), A Decade in the Dugout (Dynasty seasons, `byMode.dynasty`, 3 / 6 / 10), Old School (classic-season titles, before 2008-09, never a Super League side, board, 1 / 5 / 15), Moneyball (titles under a salary cap, board, 1 / 3 / 10), Perfect Day (perfect Dailies from `dailyResults`, 1 / 3 / 10), Summit (Gauntlets completed, 1 / 3 / 10), The Long Road (32-club Cups, 1 / 3 / 10), Under the Lights (Daily Arena days, 7 / 30 / 100), Hot Seat (hot-seat and online head-to-head seasons, 1 / 10 / 30), Old Hand (seasons played, 10 / 50 / 200), Completist (achievements unlocked, 10 / 20 / all); stamps from their achievements: Golden Boot (a 30-goal top scorer, `boot30`), The Wall (`glove20`), Chain Gang (`chain-champions`), One-Club Wonder (`one-club-champions`). Every league won is the Globetrotter's gold (7). The manager card keeps the twelve engine badges.

- **The r8 feats and milestones** (`Cabinet.feats?: CabinetFeats`, optional; `src/game/logic/feats.ts`, pure, no RNG, nothing written to a game). Counts the newer milestones need that nothing kept before: engine 7's Continental Cup groups topped and group stages won with every match won (`seasonGroupRead` on a Season or Dynasty's `continental.groups`, `quickGroupRead` on a quick run's `groups`, the engine's own order: points, goal difference, goals), two-legged ties won after losing the first leg (`seasonComebacks` from `CupTie.firstLeg`, `quickComebacks` from the bracket's `legs`), the longest Dynasty (`dynastyBest`, a maximum, never a sum), academy graduates (the summer's `youthIntake.chosen` still in the squad at the season's end), titles by real season (`seasonsTitled`, distinct start years, never a Super League side), classic titles (before 2008-09), titles under a salary cap and thrifty ones (a quarter of the cap or more unspent, `THRIFT_SHARE`; a Dynasty's later seasons are not capped), perfect drafts (every pick graded A by `pickGrades`, worked out by the store in `isPerfectDraft`, `src/game/logic/perfectDraft.ts`; never with the case's Icon; filed by seed so the same wheel counts once) and Run-in of the week briefs met (by week key, once). `recordResult` credits a league season's feats with `creditGameFeats` straight after `creditGame`, under the same once-per-result guard, and a run-in's with `creditRunInFeatsOn` after `creditRunIn`; the store's `playCup` a quick season's post-season cup through `creditCupFeats`, once per `resultId:comp` (`seen`). `cabinet.ts` itself is unchanged: the edge verifier's bundle reaches it through `quickCups.ts`, and any change to its symbols renames the bundle, so `feats.ts` wraps it. Every list holds distinct keys, at most `FEAT_LIST_CAP` (200). A cabinet from before the feats keeps none until the persist `merge` (or the next credit, through `cabinetWithFeats`: `normaliseCabinet`, or `rebuildCabinet` for a profile before the Trophy Room, then `withFeats`) catches it up once from the leaderboard and the run-in log (`featsFromBoard`: titles by season, classic and capped titles, the longest Dynasty by entries sharing a seed, met weekly run-ins); `withFeats` also normalises feats already there. Group stages, comebacks, the academy and perfect drafts start from zero on an older save. Thirteen milestones read them (bronze / silver / gold): Group Winners (1 / 5 / 15), Comeback Kings (1 / 3 / 10), Dynasty Builder (seasons in one Dynasty, 5 / 10 / 20), Academy Graduates (1 / 5 / 15), Through the Ages (different real seasons won in, 3 / 10 / 25), Bargain Hunter (1 / 3 / 10), Perfect Draft (1 / 5 / 15), Online Victor (online rooms won, `h2hOnline.won`, 1 / 10 / 30), Arena Champion (Daily Arena days won outright from the local `arenaDaily.results`: the 16-club Cup lifted or all 15 rungs, 1 / 5 / 20), Weekly Winner (Weekly Challenge weeks ending in a title, the first result filed for each week, 1 / 4 / 12), Firefighter (Run-in of the week briefs met, the cabinet's weeks and the log's, 1 / 4 / 12); stamps: Six from Six (a perfect group stage) and Class of '92 (a title in 1992-93). Old School and Moneyball now read the cabinet's counts, the board's a floor. Not counted: a hot-seat win (both managers are on the same device), and online rooms only per seat and device, once (`RoomSession`'s `credited`); someone with two devices could still play themselves. Contract renewals have no badge: nothing records a renewal once its season ends.
- **The unlock moment** (display only; `src/game/logic/trophyChase.ts`, `src/ui/components/BadgeUnlocks.tsx`, `src/ui/trophyReveal.ts`). `trophyItems` puts the badges and milestones in one list; `earnedKeys` names every earned tier (`id:tier`, lower tiers included); `freshUnlocks` compares them with the per-viewer key `touchline-trophies-revealed` (best tier first; a viewer with no record and more than `REVEAL_BASELINE` (3) earned keys takes them as the baseline silently, so an older career is not one big reveal). The Quick, Season, Dynasty, arena and run-in summaries show a Trophy Room card while something is new (`badge-unlocks`): each new medal pops in on the spring curve (`unlock-pop`, `--dur-4`, transform and opacity, off under reduced motion), its tier and count, a Share button (`shareOrCopy`, English text from `unlockShareText`), and Next to chase (`nextToChase`: started tiered badges short of gold nearest their next threshold, never a stamp), Got it files them as seen; Trophy Room opens the room, which marks them New and files them itself. The medal's ring sounds through the Trophy Room's own `useBadgeSound`. The Trophy Room shows Next to chase above the badges and marks a tile New (`.badge-tile.fresh`) on the visit that files it as seen.

  | Badge | Counts | Bronze / silver / gold |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | Serial Winner | league titles | 1 / 5 / 20 |
  | Centurion | 100-point seasons | 1 / 5 / 15 |
  | Perfectionist | perfect seasons | 1 / 3 / 10 |
  | Cup Collector | season cups plus arena cups | 1 / 5 / 20 |
  | Globetrotter | leagues won in | 2 / 4 / 7 |
  | International | nations titled in one-nation drafts | 1 / 3 / 8 |
  | Grounds Tour | different clubs titled | 5 / 15 / 40 |
  | Regular | daily challenges | 7 / 30 / 100 |
  | Sunday League | weekly challenges | 4 / 12 / 52 |
  | Ironman | rungs in the best Gauntlet | 5 / 10 / 15 |
  | Giant Killer | arena wins on Realistic over sides with real team strength 85+ | 1 / 10 / 50 |
  | Survival Artist | run-in briefs met | 1 / 5 / 20 |

- **Profile page.** The Trophy cabinet row adds 38-0s, Invincibles and Centurions to the five trophies; the arena's cups and best Gauntlet and the run-in's briefs met sit on their own shelves beside them, never in these totals. The Trophy Room panel (above the achievements) shows every badge with its tier ring and progress, the leagues won in, and "Download manager card". Best seasons has a filter, All · Quick and Daily · Season · Dynasty (`filterBoard`, `boardGroup`), remembered per browser under `touchline-record-filter`; arena runs have Best runs and run-ins the Run-ins table.
- **Manager card** (`renderManagerCard`, `src/ui/shareCard.ts`, 1080 by 1350 PNG): the manager name and seasons simulated, a 3 by 2 grid of trophy counts (league titles, cups, Continental Cups, doubles, trebles, perfect seasons), the top six badges with their tier rings (`topBadges`: highest tier, then nearest the next tier, then table order), the three best seasons, the data stamp and the TOUCHLINE footer.

### The quick season: kick-off, playback, January and Europe

`src/game/logic/quick.ts`, `src/game/logic/january.ts`, `src/game/logic/odds.ts`, `src/ui/screens/KickOff.tsx`, `src/ui/components/PreSeasonOdds.tsx`, `src/ui/screens/SeasonPlayback.tsx`. After the draft of a Quick Draft or the Daily the season is no longer instant.

- **Phases.** `finishDraft` calls `toKickoff`: the XI is set (`quickXI`, the draft's lineup with empty slots filled by `autoPickLineup`) and `Game.phase` becomes `kickoff`. `kickOff(game, u, skip)` plays the whole season (`runQuickSeason`) or, with `GameSettings.january`, the first half only (`openJanuary`, phase `january`); `closeJanuary(game, u, index | null)` takes one listing or passes and plays the season out. Phase `summary` then follows as before. `Phase` gained `kickoff` and `january`; older saves never hold either, and `normaliseGame` sends a save in `january` without its `quickJanuary` back to `kickoff`, from which the same season replays.
- **Squad complete and the pre-season odds** (`src/game/logic/odds.ts`, `SquadComplete`): the kick-off screen opens on a "Squad complete" panel. `oddsLeague` builds the league about to be played (the XI as `quickXI` fields it with the manager's tactics and captain, the AI clubs with the season's calibration) and works out every home-and-away pairing's expected goals once, with the engine's own pre-match sums (`pairingExpectation` in `match.ts`: strengths, tactical modifiers, marking, set pieces, aerial power and leadership, with no match-day variance and no team talk; `beginMatch` calls the same helpers, and draws exactly what it drew before). `sampleSeasons` then plays `ODDS_SEASONS = 2000` seasons from those numbers: each fixture draws both sides' match-day variance (log-normal with the engine's consistency spread), puts the day's expected goals under the engine's soft ceiling and 0.12 floor (`matchDayXg`; until 2026-10-28 it clamped at engine 5's 5.8, which overstated an elite XI's perfect and unbeaten chances) and draws Poisson goals, and the table sorts on points, goal difference, goals scored and club id as `computeTable` does. That is about a thousandth of the cost of playing every match through the engine, and over six seeds the mean expected points sit within a few points of the seasons the engine plays out (tested). The odds draw from `Rng.fromState(quickStartRng ?? rngState).fork('pre-season-odds')`, a fork of a copy, so the same squad and tactics always show the same odds and the season itself never moves; nothing is saved. The panel shows the projected finish (the median place), expected points (the mean), the chance to win the league, finish top 4, top 6 and top 10 and go down (bottom three), printed as "52.6%", "<0.1%", "0%" (`chanceLabel`), and the XI's Overall, Attack, Midfield, Defence and Goalkeeper ratings as bars (`squadBreakdown`, `barFill`: 50 empty, 95 full; numbers shown in blind mode too: the XI is the user's own). The UI samples `ODDS_STEP = 250` seasons per idle moment (`usePreSeasonOdds`, `requestIdleCallback` where there is one) and caches the odds by `oddsKey` (the game id, start state, XI, bench, tactics, captain, league, difficulty and draft rules), so a tactics change works them out again after a short pause; the chance and unit bars grow in one after another once the odds land. Display only: no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump.
- **The tactics step**: folded under the Squad complete panel ("Tactics, captain and penalties"), the drafted XI on a read-only pitch, the captain and the penalty taker (selects over the XI, "Auto" by default), and the six team instructions with the Dugout's labels and help (mentality, tempo, pressing, defensive line, width, passing; `TeamInstructions` in `src/ui/components/TacticsControls.tsx`). The big "Simulate season" button plays with them; "Simulate with the default tactics" (shown with the tactics) puts `defaultTactics` back and clears the captain first. The quick season's user side is `quickUserInput`: the XI with `Game.tactics`, `Game.captainId` and `tactics.setPieces.penalties`, so every choice reaches the match engine. With the defaults it is exactly the input the instant season always used.
- **One stream, split at the halfway matchweek.** `generateFixtures` returns the fixtures in matchweek order, so playing matchweeks 1 to 19 and then 20 to 38 draws exactly what one loop draws. The window opens after `matchweeks / 2` (19 of 38, 17 of 34). `openJanuary` writes nothing to `Game.rngState`: once the window closes `runQuickSeason` plays the whole season again from the same state, the first half with the tactics and captain it kicked off with (`QuickJanuary.firstHalfTactics`, `firstHalfCaptainId`) and the second half with the current ones. Passing with unchanged tactics therefore gives the instant season figure for figure, the RNG state included (tested over three seeds, and against `runQuickSeason` called straight after the draft), and it needed no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump (it stayed `'3'`).
- **The window** (`QuickJanuary`, saved on the game): the position after the first half, a budget of the XI's combined value times a share rising evenly from 8% for the leaders to 20% for the bottom side, at least £5M, in £0.5M steps (`januaryBudget`), and three to five listings (`januaryListings`). The listings shop for the three weakest slots by the drafted player's rating at the slot's position (`weakestSlots`, Prime-aware through `playerLookup`); candidates are every player-season the wheel could have offered (its leagues, era, one club or one nation) who plays that position naturally and whose player is not already in the squad (the XI or the bench, so a substitute never meets himself on the pitch). Every listing but the last is a signing, rated 3 to 15 above the man he replaces, with a fee of his value in £0.5M steps within the budget; the last is a straight swap, no fee, 0 to 5 above. Slots cycle through the three weakest and a slot with no candidate passes to the next, so a narrow wheel can offer fewer. Each listing is drawn from the 25 best candidates for its slot, one season per player (his best that fits the band and the budget, `januaryShortlist`, so one man's many seasons, all alike under Prime, never fill the list), all draws from a fork of a copy of the season's stream (`Rng.fromState(state).fork('january')`), which leaves the season itself untouched. The card says the deal "can help or hurt, and there is no undo"; the deal replaces the man in that slot for the second half, and he gives up the armband and any set piece. `runQuickSeason` recomputes the window and refuses a deal that was not on offer; should that happen to the manager's own save (the league data updated while it sat in January), the store draws the window again from the start of the season with the first-half tactics (`reopenJanuary`) and says so, instead of leaving the season stuck. A season kicked off by an earlier engine (a save across an engine release) is caught before anything replays it (`quickSeam.ts`: a copy played again does not give the games on show): an open window is drawn again on this engine with that reason, and a bench change after the window is refused, so a game already shown never changes its score. `QuickResult.january` keeps the names for the summary's "January business" panel and the share line `January: signed Rice (£45M), Fernandes out` or `January: swapped Fernandes for Rice`. The tactics can be changed at the same pause ("Second-half tactics"). The pause opens on a "Halfway there" panel under the kicker "January transfer window": W-D-L, Points and GD boxes over the first half and one pace line (`paceLine`, `pointsPace` in `gameByGame.ts`: the place the side is on course for and the points it would end on at its rate so far, with the title, the top four or the bottom three named when it is there), then "Enter the transfer market" (the budget and the listings, a deal, "Stick with your XI" or Back) and "Stick with your XI" (a pass).
- **The January gamble** (a Quick Draft only, `januaryGambleOn`: mode `quick` and not a `weekly-` seed; `src/game/logic/januaryGamble.ts`, `rollJanuarySpin` / `rollJanuaryUpgrade` / `closeJanuarySpin` in `quick.ts`, `src/ui/components/JanuaryWindow.tsx`). The "Halfway there" panel offers four boxes instead of two: **January spin**, **January Upgrader**, **Transfer market** (the listings above) and **Skip** (a pass). One deal per window: once the case is open or the dial rolled, the others are gone (`januaryGambleMade`). *The spin*: its pool is every player the wheel could have offered (its leagues, era, classic gate, club and nation rules), once at his best season through the game's lookup (Prime counts), ranked by overall and cut into the reel's tiers by the club-season shares (best 5% gold, 10% red, 20% pink, 30% purple, 35% blue), less anyone whose player is in the squad (bench included) or who cannot cover a slot of the shape at the draft's minimum fit (`januarySpinPool`). The draw is a roll over whole-per-cent tier weights `JANUARY_SPIN_WEIGHTS` (gold 3, red 9, pink 20, purple 30, blue 38; an empty tier drops out and the odds shown are the weight over the live total, `januarySpinOdds`), then one player of the tier evenly (`drawJanuarySpin`; an 8,000-draw test checks the frequencies against the odds). The panel shows the odds and an idle reel; "Open the January case" stores the landing (`QuickJanuary.spin = { playerSeasonId, tier }`) and the item reel slides onto him (fillers from the pool as often as their tier's odds), then his card and the slots he can take, the biggest gain first (`januarySpinChoices`: the slot, the man making way, both ratings there), "Bring X in for Y" or "Keep my XI". He replaces that starter from matchweek 20 on and the starter drops any armband or set piece. *The Upgrader*: `UpgraderStage` on the XI at the pause, the rating the game's lookup gives (`januaryUpgradeBase`), the engine's `upgradeChance`; the roll is `drawJanuaryUpgrade` (`QuickJanuary.upgrade = { id, boost, chance, roll, won, delta }`, as `DraftUpgrade`), and "Play the second half" plays on with `withJanuaryUpgrade` on the lookup of the second half only (the first half replays as played), and on the post-season cups (`seasonEndLookup`). *Determinism*: the spin draws from `Rng.fromState(halfway).fork('january-spin')` and the Upgrader from `.fork('january-upgrade').fork('<id>:<boost>')`, both forks of a copy of the season's stream at the halfway mark (the copy the listings fork), so `Game.rngState` never moves and every season without a gamble is the season it was (no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump); `runQuickSeason` re-draws the spin and re-rolls the upgrade and refuses one that does not match (`applyJanuaryGamble`); the highlights replay and the bench panel apply it too. `QuickResult.januaryGamble` keeps the names for the summary's "January spin" / "January Upgrader" panel and the share line (`januaryGambleLine`: `🎰 January spin: Lacroix in for Kaboul`, `⬆️ January Upgrader: Cole -5 (lost at 16%)`). A challenge link carries it as `x.jg` (`['s', slot]`, `['s']` for a spin whose player was not taken, `['u', id, boost]`; never with `x.j`), and the replay opens the same case and rolls the same roll. Old saves and links have neither field and play as before; `normaliseGame` drops a malformed one. The Daily and the weekly keep the plain window; a season with `x.jg` is non-standard for the ranked boards (`nonStandardRun`, and `unrankedReason` on the device), so a replay never ranks a different season. Local records count it like any other season.
- **Managers** (`src/engine/managers.ts`, `src/game/logic/managers.ts`, `src/ui/components/ManagerPick.tsx`; `DraftRules.managers`, `true` in `QUICK_RULES`, `false` in `DAILY_RULES`, absent in older saves and every other mode, read as off; Setup's Manager option). Once the draft is done (the test `finishDraft` makes) the Draft screen offers `managerOptions`: the real managers of the club-seasons drafted (`managersOf`, from `src/engine/data/managers.json`, DATA.md "Managers"), the club-season the draft took most players from first and then in draft order, each man once, at most six; when fewer than three, invented archetypes of styles not yet offered (one per style, starting at a place fixed by a hash of the club-seasons). `toKickoff` settles the manager (`settleManager`: the one picked if still offered, else the first), and the Squad complete screen can change him until kick-off; he is saved as `Game.manager` with his name and style. A style is worth `managerBoost(style, tactics, xi)`: pressing +1.5% own expected goals and -1.5% conceded, attacking +3% and +1% conceded, defensive -1% and -3%, set pieces +1.5% and -1%, balanced +1% and -1%, youth +0.5% and -0.5% plus up to +2.5% and -2% by the share of the XI aged 23 or under; instructions that suit the style (a high press, an attacking or a balanced mentality, a defensive mentality or a deep line, a named penalty or set-piece taker) add half a point to a point; each end is clamped to `1 ± MANAGER_EDGE` (4%). `quickUserInput` puts it on the side as `MatchTeamInput.managerBoost`, which `snapshotOf` multiplies into the side's `boost`, so the league, both cups, the pre-season odds and the highlights replay all carry it. Nothing draws from an `Rng`: with managers off, or no manager, every season, cup and RNG state is what it was before (pinned against the parent commit in `src/game/__tests__/managers.test.ts`), so managers needed no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump. Challenge links carry the manager as `x.m` (a Wikidata id or `arch-<style>`) and always write the rule (`managers: true` or `false` in the rules diff, like `difficulty`), so a link made before managers existed (no `managers` key) opens with managers off for its replay and for the friend who takes it up (`rulesFromPayload`); the replay looks him up among the managers the replayed draft offers, and a link naming one it does not offer fails to verify. The share text adds `👔 <name> · <style>`.
- **The manager spin** (`DraftRules.managerSpin`; Setup's Manager option is Spin, Pick or Off, Spin by default, which writes `managerSpin: true` beside `managers: true`; absent from `QUICK_RULES` and `DAILY_RULES`, so older saves, older links, the Daily and every test built on `QUICK_RULES` pick as before; `src/engine/managers.ts` `managerWheel`/`spinManagers`, `src/game/logic/managers.ts` `rollManagerSpin`/`managerSpinOdds`/`managerTier`, `src/ui/components/ManagerSpin.tsx`). At `toKickoff`, before `settleManager`, `rollManagerSpin` draws `MANAGER_SPIN_REELS = 3` different managers from the wheel: every real manager of every club-season the rules admit (their leagues, era, classic gate and one club; not the modes), once per club-season he ran, each entry equally likely, a man already landed skipped; archetypes fill any reel the wheel cannot. The draws come from `Rng.fromState(game.rngState).fork('manager-spin')`, a fork of a copy, so `Game.rngState` is untouched and the season is the one the same manager would play by hand (tested). The three are saved on `Game.managerSpin.options`; `quickManagerOptions` returns them, so the pick (`pickManager`, which sets `managerSpin.picked`), Squad complete's Change manager and a challenge link's `x.m` all check against them, and a replay rolls the same three from the stream the replayed draft leaves. Until the pick the season would take the first reel's man. The screen (`kickoffStep` = `managers`) is three item reels side by side spun at once, landing one after another (450 ms apart, half with Quick spin), each on a manager card in the tier of the club-season he ran (`tierTable`; an archetype is blue); the panel shows the chance of each tier per reel (`managerSpinOdds`, the wheel's share, exact for each reel's first draw). Tap, Space or Enter lands them all. Rarity is colour only: the edge is the style's, as with a picked manager.
- **The end-of-draft case and the locker** (`DraftRules.endCase`; Setup's End case card beside Power-ups, on by default; never in the Daily, which pins its rules, and not in a game played from a challenge link (`endCaseOn`), so a head-to-head compares two drafts; `src/engine/endCase.ts`, `src/game/logic/endCase.ts`, `src/ui/components/EndCase.tsx`, `Locker.tsx`). At `toKickoff` `rollEndCase` opens one case from `Rng.fromState(game.rngState).fork('end-case')`: one `int(0, 99)` against `CASE_ITEMS` (Common 54%: Peek, Swap, Lock 18% each; Rare 28%: Re-roll season, Captain's armband 14% each; Epic 13%: the Upgrader; Icon 5%), then for an Icon one `pick` among the Icons collection (`iconsCollection` over the wheel's leagues) not drafted already (by player) who can cover some slot of the shape at the draft's minimum fit; with none the roll drops the Upgrader. The case screen (`kickoffStep` = `case`, after the manager) shows the odds table first, then the reel (`openCase`). A power-up goes to `Profile.inventory` (the locker, `addToLocker`, once: `openEndCase` returns it only on the first open) and changes nothing this season. An Icon may come in for a drafted starter (`subCaseIcon`/`placeCaseIcon`: a slot in `DraftState.lineup` he can cover at `minFit`, rated there by `ratingAt` as any player in a slot is; the choices are listed with the man making way, biggest gain first; a change of mind takes the earlier choice back), or be passed up (Keep my XI). The man making way sits the season out and drops any captaincy or set-piece duty. A season with the case's Icon (`caseIconIn`) is non-standard: it never earns the points record, its leaderboard entry carries `caseIcon: true` (a ★ Case Icon chip on the board, the kick-off, the summary) and the share text says so. A challenge link carries the Icon's slot (`x.ic`); a replay rolls the same case and puts him there (a case without an Icon, or a slot he cannot take, does not verify). **The locker**: in a Quick Draft without the power-up tray (not the Daily, not a challenge link, `lockerOn`), a Locker row over the squad lists what the profile holds; Unpack takes one off the profile and adds it to `DraftState.locker` (`unpackPowerUp`), after which it sits in a tray of its own and works exactly as the tray's, once (`hasPowerUp` replaces the tray check in every `can*`; `upgraderAvailable` for the Upgrader). Re-roll season and the Upgrader get the very streams `newDraft` seeds a tray draft with (`seed:powerups`, `seed:upgrader`), from the seed alone, so when they are unpacked never matters. A link carries the unpacked kinds (`i`), unpacked before the replay's first spin; `isChallengeLocker` checks them. The reels are `ItemReel` (`src/ui/components/ItemReel.tsx`, the case reel's strip, curve, ticks and landing sounds without the season drum; strips dealt by `src/game/logic/itemReel.ts` with `Math.random` for the fillers only).
- **Quick Draft only.** The manager spin, the end-of-draft case and the locker check `settings.mode === 'quick'` (`managersOn`, `endCaseRule`, `lockerOn`), so a casual Quick Draft (Moneyball, the Teammate chain) has them and no other mode does: the Cup Final, the Cup, the Gauntlet and the Daily Arena (mode `arena`), the Run-in (`runin`) and the Weekly (`weekly`, which still stops at the kick-off step) never roll them, even with the rules set (`src/game/__tests__/casualExtras.test.ts`). Both rules are v1 rule keys, so main's links that carry them, and a link's locker `i`, still open as v1; an arena link never carries `i`.
- **The bench** (`DraftRules.benchPicks`, 0 to `QUICK_BENCH_MAX = 5`, `benchPlaces`; Setup's Bench option for Quick Draft, default 1, Off writes no field; the Daily never has one). *Drafting* (`src/engine/draft.ts`): `benchRoom` lets a Squad First pick go to the bench at any point while it has room (never while a Position First spin fills its chosen slot); the wheel pool and its weights still look only at the XI's open slots (`canUse` is unchanged; `canBench` is the extra check), so a bench changes no spin until it is used. Once the XI is full, `isComplete` waits for the bench to fill or for `closeBench` ("On to kick-off with n on the bench", a spin still showing is logged as skipped). Spins after the XI is full draw from `DraftState.benchRng`, forked off a copy of the game's stream at the first one (`fork('bench')`), so `Game.rngState`, and with it the season, is where the XI left it however many bench spins follow (tested over four seeds). Bench picks travel in challenge links as `[playerId, null]` rows and `replayDraft` closes a short bench as the player did; `benchPicks` is in `RULE_CHECKS` (0 to 5). *In the season* (`src/game/logic/quickBench.ts`, `quick.ts`): with someone on the bench, the match engine is handed no substitutes (the bench plays between games, not in them), injuries count: a user player injured in matchweek k with `weeksOut` w misses matchweeks k+1 to k+w (`QuickGameResult.injuries`, folded by `injuredThrough`), and each injured starter is covered by the best fit on the bench who can play the slot under the draft's minimum fit (`canPlayFor`; rating at the slot's position, bench order on ties, slots in formation order, `fieldXI`, `QuickGameResult.covers`); he goes back in when fit, and a starter nobody can cover plays on. A cover is a forced change and uses none of the `QUICK_CHANGES = 3` voluntary ones. A voluntary change (`QuickChange { from, slotId, inId }`, `Game.quickChanges`, at most three, in order) puts a bench player into a slot he can play (the same fit rule as the draft) from matchweek `from` on, the starter there taking his place on the bench (`applyChange`), and sticks until changed again. It is made from the playback, before the next game: `makeQuickChange` appends it and plays the season again from the state it kicked off from (`Game.quickStartRng`), so every game already shown comes out the same and only what follows can change. In phase `january` only first-half changes are taken, and the window is drawn again (`reopenJanuary`); once the window has closed only second-half ones, applied after the deal. `quickChangeProblem` says why a change is refused (no bench, none left, the season over, before the last change, the January call first, an injured or absent player, one out of position). A season with a bench is kept open (`Game.quickOpen`) while the playback runs, and the store records it (leaderboard, achievements, the Daily) once when the playback closes (`endPlayback`, `closeQuickSeason`), or when the manager leaves it for another game or abandons it (`settleQuickSeason` in `startGame`, `resumeGame` and `abandonGame`, so a season played is never lost from the record); a reload lands back on the playback's last frame until then, and `runQuickSeason` works its unlocks out afresh on each run (`QUICK_UNLOCKS`). Without anyone on the bench the season is played exactly as before benches existed, and with a bench but no change and no cover it draws exactly what that season draws (tested: the results agree game for game up to the first cover). `QuickResult.changes` keeps each change with the starter it sent to the bench, for the summary's Bench panel ("n of 3 changes, injury cover in m games") and the share line `🔁 Bench: Rice for Fernandes (MW 12)`; challenge links carry the changes as `x.s` (`[from, slotId, inId]`, at most three), and a change that does not fit the replayed squad makes the claim unverified. The Kick-off screen lists the bench, and the playback shows a "For matchweek n" panel: the XI for the next game on a pitch (covers in place), the bench with who is injured or covering, "n of 3 changes left", and Bring on, then a tap on the starter he replaces.
- **The playback** (`SeasonPlayback`, `useSeasonPlayback`): every result is computed before the first game shows; only the reveal is paced, one game every `PLAYBACK_STEP_MS = 1500` ms at 1x (a calm pace: the result lands and the tally counts up before the next), 3 s at 0.5x, 750 ms at 2x and 375 ms at 4x (`PLAYBACK_SPEEDS = [0.5, 1, 2, 4]`, `playbackDelay`; until 2026-10-25 700, 350 and 175 ms with no 0.5x), starting on its own. The chosen speed is remembered per viewer in `localStorage['touchline-season-speed']` (`parsePlaybackSpeed`, inside try/catch; anything else reads as 1x), apart from the match screen's own speed. Each game drops in at the top of the game-by-game list (newest first; every row of a newest-first list lands with the same short drop and flash, so the row a new game pushes down keeps its animation and never fades in twice; the newest row is a little larger), under a running tally (`SeasonTally`): Won, Drawn, Lost and Points boxes that count up to the new value over about 350 ms as each result lands (`countUpValue`, an ease-out, display only), then "GF 4 · GA 5 · GD -1" (`goalDifferenceLabel`) and the position (`playbackFrame`, `nextReveal`, pure and tested). `prefers-reduced-motion` shows every value at once and animates no row. The header reads "Matchweek n / 38", counting the season's games from `quickSeasonGames` (every other club twice, so a bye week in an odd-sized league is never counted). Pause, the speed and "Skip to January" or "Skip to the end" are UI state. In phase `january` it stops after the first half on the window card; after the deal it carries on from game 20; at the end "See the summary" opens the summary. Where the playback stands is transient store state (`playback: { from }`, never saved): a reload during the first half lands on the window, a reload after the season on the summary. The last weeks of a race wait longer (round 9, `src/ui/seasonDrama.ts`, display only): within the last `RACE_GAMES` (6) games, while the user leads by no more than the games left could close and at most `RACE_GAP.title` (6) points, or trails the leader by no more than that, or sits in or just above the bottom three within `RACE_GAP.drop` (4) points of the line, the wait before each game is the step times `RACE_PACE` (1.2 six games out, then 1.3, 1.45, 1.6, 1.8 and 2.1 on the final day), never more than `RACE_EXTRA_MAX_MS` (2.5 s) longer; a chip names the race and the games left ("Title race: 3 games to go", "Final day"), and the next fixture's card waits at the top of the list. A result can also flare (`flareAt`): a points milestone (50, 75, 90, 100), a goals milestone (50, 75, 100), a run of wins (3, 5, 7, then every fifth; not while the season is still perfect, which the moment chip marks), an unbeaten run from 10 in fives, clean sheets from 3, or a rout (won by four or more), the heaviest only and only when no moment shows. Nothing of it reads or moves the game's generator.
- **The playback's sound** (`src/ui/sound/seasonCues.ts`, pure; `usePlaybackSounds.ts`; the cues in `docs/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` section 14, "The season's cues"): a quiet blip as each result lands (W, D, L), a small cheer for two goals or more, a stinger on the game that brings a big moment (the title sealed or the drop certain from `clinchMatchweeks` over the season's rounds, then `momentAt`'s perfect run over, top, top four and every fifth perfect win; the heaviest only), a low crowd bed under the last five games of a perfect run, a chime when the January window opens and a short fanfare by the final place. At 4x it thins (shorter blips at least 700 ms apart unless the outcome changes, no cheer or bed), Skip plays one riffle and the closing cue, and a pause or the highlights stop it. Season and Dynasty (`seasonWatch.ts`, reading the store only): one result cue for Play matchweek on Sim result, one riffle for Sim 5, Sim to end or Sim to the next big match, with the title or relegation stinger; a watched or auto-played week is the match day's, and a season ending with the title leaves it to the summary's fanfare. Presentation only, under Sound.
- **Highlights of any game** (`src/engine/highlights.ts`, `src/game/logic/highlights.ts`, `src/ui/components/Highlights.tsx`): each row of the game-by-game list (the playback's and the summary's) carries a Highlights button. The quick season keeps only scores and scorers, so the game is rebuilt rather than stored: `QuickResult.startRng` keeps the RNG state the season kicked off from (in phase `january`, `Game.rngState` still is that state), and `replayUserMatch` plays the season again from it with the same sides, first-half tactics and captain (`QuickJanuary.firstHalfTactics` once a window opened), January deal and bench changes, stopping at the user's game of that matchweek and handing back both team inputs, the summary result and the state just before the match. Nothing on the game moves. `quickHighlights` refuses (null) when the rebuilt score, side or opponent differs from the list (league data changed under the save), and older saves without `startRng` show no button. A summary-detail match logs goals, cards, injuries and substitutions but no near misses, so `summaryHighlights` keeps the match's own events (the scoreline is the season's) and draws up to two reel chances or saves per side per half (Poisson on the side's xG share, shooter weighted by position, the full engine's `CHANCE_TEXTS`), the shot positions (`placeEvents`) and the momentum (`momentumBins`) from `Rng.fromState(before).fork('highlights-<mw>')`, a fork of a copy, so the reel is identical on every replay and never touches a draw of the season; appearances come from the XI and the events (`appearancesFromEvents`: a substitute takes the slot of the man he replaced, a red card or an injury ends a man's game). The moments are goals, penalties, own goals, chances, saves, bookings and sendings-off in minute order, each with the score after it. The reel (`HighlightsModal`, a bottom sheet under 720 px) runs the shared live clock through a two-minute build-up to each moment, 300 ms a minute except the minute before the moment (1,800 ms) and the moment's own (2,600 ms; `highlightMinuteMs`), so the move that makes it reads (its ball path starts with a keeper rolling it out), holds 1.7 s on it with the commentary line and the scoreline, then moves on; Pause, Next moment, Skip to full time, a full-time list of every moment (tap one to watch it again), Close and Escape. Opening it pauses the playback (on the summary, a running replay is shown in full first); the sheet opens at once with a holding line and the game is rebuilt after that first paint (a late game replays most of the season). Full time sits on the last event, so a stoppage-time goal is on the pitch at the end. A bench change that no longer fits the squad on the data loaded gives null, not a crash. Tested: every game of plain, January (with a deal and tactics changed in the window, and while the window is open) and bench (with a change) seasons rebuilds to the score and scorer minutes shown, twice alike, the save untouched; goals and cards are all there; every shot is by a man on the pitch.
  - **One main call** (round 5, `summaryMainCall` in `src/game/logic/summaryCall.ts`): while a cup is still on offer its Play button is the summary's one primary (the Cup first, as the cards stand) and Copy result is secondary; once neither is open Copy result is primary again. Skip it passes a cup on offer even when its field is not loaded yet (`passQuickCup`; `skipQuickCup` refused the Continental Cup with only the wheel's league loaded, so the button only toasted).
