  🟩 a rung won in 90 minutes, 🟨 won on penalties, 🟥 the defeat (a lost shoot-out included). The scorer is the run's top scorer in the squad and the star the man with the most man-of-the-match awards.

### The Cup

A knockout of 8, 16 or 32 clubs, the user's side among them, in one sitting against real club-seasons; the one-off final is the Cup's two-club size. Draft sixteen whatever the size (`cupGameSettings`: a final's settings with `arena: { format: 'cup', size, field, decade? }`, 16 clubs unless another size is chosen).

- **Fields** (`cupField`, `src/engine/cup.ts`, `size − 1` entrants from `streams.field`, one draw each, from pools sorted by key; the user's draft never enters it):
  - **The wheel**: one club-season per club, each a season uniformly and then a club by the wheel's weighting (the final's draw, repeated without a club already in).
  - **Champions**: the data's champions, uniformly without replacement (every league with a table).
  - **Giants**: the strongest 5% of the pool, uniformly without replacement (every league).
  - **A decade** (`settings.arena.decade`, 2000 for 2008-09 and 2009-10, 2010, 2020): club-seasons of the wheel's leagues in that decade with a real team strength of 78 or more (`DECADE_STRENGTH`). The decade replaces the era for the field; the wheel keeps the era.
  - **Europe this season**: the top four (`EUROPE_PER_LEAGUE`) of every league's latest complete season by final position (by real strength for a league without a table), uniformly without replacement; a field larger than the candidates (a 32-club cup against seven leagues' 28) takes them all and the strongest of each league's fifth places. No era. It needs every pack, as the champions and the giants do (`arenaNeedsAllLeagues`, `leaguesFor`).

  The champions and giants fields allow a club in several seasons (Real Madrid 2016-17 can meet Real Madrid 2017-18). When a field runs dry (a narrow era, England's 18 champions for a 32-club cup), the strongest unused club-seasons of its pool fill the rest. A one-off final against a decade or Europe is that field with one entrant.
- **Seeds and the draw.** The seeds are the strongest quarter of the field by real strength (`cupSeeds`: 2 of 8, 4 of 16, 8 of 32); the user is never seeded. Round one (`drawFirstRound`, a fork of `streams.draw`) shuffles both lists and draws every seed against an unseeded side first, then pairs the unseeded sides left, so you meet a seed in round one one time in three at 16 and 32 clubs (4 of 12 and 8 of 24). Each later round is redrawn from the round's winners in bracket order (`drawNextRound`, one fork of `streams.draw` a round). A coin from the draw picks every home side; the final is on neutral ground.
- **Rounds** (`cupRoundName`, `cupImportance`): Round of 32 (`R32`), Round of 16 (`R16`), Quarter-final (`QF`), Semi-final (`SF`), Cup final (`F`); importance 1.1 for the first round whatever it is and for the round of 16, 1.15 a quarter-final, 1.25 a semi-final, 1.4 the final. Level at 90 goes to penalties (no extra time): the user's with the kick-by-kick shoot-out, the AI sides' with the Season cups' team-overall `shootout()`.
- **Calibration.** One offset for the whole field (`bracket.offset`, `knockoutOffset` over every entrant's real strength against `CUP_FIELD_TARGET`), taken off every AI side in every tie: the final's targets except the wheel, which the Cup's balance guard moved from 72 to 73.5. 0 on Realistic.
- **AI ties.** Played at summary detail when the user's tie of the round is decided, in bracket order, each on its own fork of `streams.ties` labelled `${round}:${position}` (the user's position is forked too, so where he sits never moves the others). AI state carries between rounds: the result goes onto both sides under the arena's card rules (`applyArenaResult`: a red card and every second booking of the run are one-match bans, served by sitting the next tie out), and `bracket.states` keeps, for the clubs still alive only, the player states that differ from a fresh arena side's, so a giant can reach the final without its suspended striker and a 32-club cup's save stays small. A side that beats the user plays on with its legs and bans.
- **Between ties ("four days").** `arenaRecovery(game, u, 0.6)` for the user's squad (60% of a week's recovery plus the training focus, one tie off every injury) and `recoverCupStates` for every stored AI state; one training focus a tie (chosen on the hub).
- **After you go out** the rest of the bracket is recovered, drawn and played round by round to its winner (`bracket.winner`), so the summary names it. Everyone on a seed who reaches round k has met the same opponents and meets the same one there.
- **Tiers** (`arenaTier`, by the round the run ended in): `CUP WINNERS`, `FINALISTS`, `SEMI-FINALISTS`, `QUARTER-FINALISTS`, `OUT IN THE ROUND OF 16`, `OUT IN THE FIRST ROUND` (a 32-club cup's round of 32). The score is ties won. **Achievements:** `cup-night` (Lift the Cup), `cup-32` (The Long Road: win a 32-club Cup), `giant-slayers` (win the Cup against the giants field) and `clean-run` (win the Cup without conceding a goal in play). **Cabinet:** `cups.runs`, `cups.won` and `cups.bySize` on the arena's shelf (`creditArena`), never a season cup.
- **Calibration, measured** (England packs of 2026-10-25, engine `'4'`, an auto-drafted 4-3-3 sixteen, assistant management, an England wheel unless stated, the giants and champions from the England pool, 1,000 seeds `cup-review-0` to `-999`, each share good to about ±2.5 points):

  | Field and size | Target | Lifted | Out in round one | Level at 90 |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | The wheel, 8 | 73.5 | 33.7% | 31.8% | 20% |
  | The wheel, 16 | 73.5 | 19.9% | 35.4% | 21% |
  | The wheel, 32 | 73.5 | 12.1% | 33.5% | 21% |
  | The wheel, 16, Realistic | none | 2.3% | 56.9% | 22% |
  | Champions, 16 | 76 | 14.0% | 43.6% | 23% |
  | Giants, 16 | 78 | 11.8% | 47.6% | 24% |
  | Giants, 16, Realistic | none | 0.0% | 86.2% | 14% |
  | The 2010s, 16 | 74 | 20.6% | 34.8% | 22% |
  | The 2020s, 16 | 74 | 21.5% | 32.8% | 21% |
  | Europe this season, 16 (all seven packs) | 75 | 12.4% | 39.5% | 23% |

  The giants of all seven leagues on Realistic: 0.0%. At the final's wheel target of 72 the 16-club wheel Cup was lifted 31.7% of the time over 300 seeds (the guard's ceiling is 32%), at 73 23.7%, at 73.5 21.7% and at 74 19.3%, so 73.5 brings it back to the plan's probe (21.0%). The decade (74) and Europe (75) targets were not probed by the plan and land inside their guard as set. The balance guard (`src/game/__tests__/cup.test.ts`, 300 assistant runs `cup-guard-0` to `-299`): the 16-club wheel lifted in [12%, 32%] (21.7%), the giants on Realistic under 3% (0.3%), the 2010s and Europe in [6%, 30%] (21.7% and 11.7%).
- **Screens.** Home's Arena row has The Cup card (`10 minutes · lift a trophy tonight`) in place of the Cup Final card. `ArenaSetup` on the Cup: the size (Final · 8 clubs · 16 clubs · 32 clubs, 16 by default), the field (The wheel, Champions, Giants, A decade with its select, Europe this season, with hints: giants are brutal on Realistic, Europe loads all seven leagues; a bigger Cup opens on the wheel, the final on the giants), the summary line ("The Cup · 16 clubs against the wheel") and "Draft for the Cup"; the Draft's finish button reads "On to the draw". The hub: the tie card ("Round of 16 · round 1 of 4 · a seed · home"), the training focus beside the team talk, a "The draw" tab with the bracket (`src/ui/components/Bracket.tsx`, taking `rounds`, `ties`, `nameOf`, `badgeOf`, `userId` and the seeds: every round side by side, stacked on a phone, your tie outlined, the side through in bold, a round not yet drawn says so), and Manage this one, "Sim this tie" and Sim to the end. The match screen's continue reads "Next: Quarter-final →". The summary: the banner with "🏆 Won by …" when it was not you, every tie, the run's scorers, the final's man of the match, the bracket, the share text and card. Dugout's `CupTab` is untouched. Share text:

  ```
  TouchLine · The Cup · 16 clubs · Champions
  Touchline XI · 4-3-3 · Premier League wheel
  🏆 CUP WINNERS
  R16 🟩 2-1 Bayern Munich 2012-13
  QF 🟨 1-1 Juventus 2015-16 (4-3 pens)
  SF 🟩 3-0 Chelsea 2016-17
  F 🟩 2-1 Real Madrid 2017-18
  ⭐ Final MOTM: K. De Bruyne
  Seed K7Q2-MX9A
  ```

  The icon is 🏆 for the winners, 🥈 for the finalists and 🏟️ otherwise; a run that went out before the final adds `🏆 Won by …` after its ties (a lost final already names the winner, and the card keeps to six lines), and the man of the match line appears only when you reached the final. A man of the match from the other side carries his club, as on the summary and the card (`⭐ Final MOTM: Savinho (Man City)`, the club's short name, or the club-season's when its league is not loaded).

### Arena challenge links and best runs

"Challenge a friend" for the arena: the friend gets your seed, rules and format, so they climb the same ladder or face the same bracket and see your result at every step.

- **Payload** (`ArenaChallengePayload` in `src/engine/challenge.ts`, always `v: 2`, `kind: 'arena'`): `s` as a quick link (seed, formation, league, wheel leagues, colours club, season, difficulty, the rules diff); `arena` = `{ f: 'gauntlet' | 'cup', n?: size, fd?: field, dc?: decade, dl?: the Daily Arena's day }`; `p` = every draft history row in order, the Gauntlet's bonus spins included; `t` = one training letter per step (`TRAINING_CODE`: b balanced, a attacking, d defensive, s set pieces, f fitness, r recovery, t tactical); `lf` = the rung the lifeline was used at; `auto` = no manager decision during the run (`!handManaged`); `ac` = the claim `{ s: steps won, w: the trophy or the top of the ladder, g: [gf, ga] or [gf, ga, pens us, pens them] per step }`; `k` = `historyKey` of every row. `isArenaLink` checks the format, size and field against the whitelists (a Gauntlet takes no size and no field but the wheel; a decade only with the decade field; `dl` a date), at most `ARENA_LINK_STEPS_MAX = 20` steps, each score an integer from 0 to 30, a level score only with its shoot-out (a level arena match always goes to penalties) and a shoot-out never level itself, a claim a finished run could make (`isRunClaim`: every step but the last won, `s` equal to the steps won, the trophy exactly when the last step was won, which only the fifteenth rung or the Cup's final can be), one known training letter per step, a lifeline only in a Gauntlet and within the steps played, and `auto` a boolean. Measured over 100 assistant runs each (England wheel, 4-3-3, name `Minh`, `https://touchline.quest/` in front): a Gauntlet link is 859 characters at the median and 998 at most (71 of the 100 runs drafted bonus spins, the longest climbed all 15 rungs), a Cup of 16 833 and 858; a made-up worst case (fifteen shoot-outs, four bonus spins and two discarded spins on top of a real draft, a 24-character name) stays under 2,000, which a test keeps.
- **Writer.** `arenaChallengeFromGame(game, u, name)` (`src/game/logic/challenge.ts`) packs a finished run (phase `summary`); null before. The claim and verdict helpers are pure, in `src/game/logic/arenaClaim.ts` (`arenaClaimOf`, `claimStep`, `claimWon`, `arenaVerdictLine`, `arenaVerdictOf`, `ghostStepLine`, `claimLine`, `challengeHeadline`), so the share text in `arena.ts` reads them without importing the link code.
- **Replay** (`replayArenaChallenge`, which `replayChallenge` dispatches to): `createGame` from the link (`settingsFromArenaChallenge`, mode `arena`, every choice fixed but the team name; a Daily Arena link plays as an ordinary run on that seed, off the streak), the draft's rows through `spin`/`pick` until the draft may close (`replayRow`, `draftClosed`), `beginArena`, then for each step the training focus from `t`, the lifeline at `lf`, the assistant's match (`playArenaStep(game, u, false)`, penalties with the default takers), its score compared with the claim, `closeArenaMatch`, and after rungs 2, 4, 6 and 8 the bonus spin's rows through `spin`/`pick` and `beginArena` again. At the end the step count, steps won and the trophy must match, every row must have been used and `historyKey` must equal `k`. The verdict:
  - `auto: true`: `'yes'` when every figure matches, `'no'` naming the first step that differs ("step 2 (Rung 2) replays to 2-1, not 3-1").
  - `auto: false`: `'hand'` once the first draft replays, read as "Managed by hand: the draft and the ladder replay, the matches are Minh's word". A hand-managed run is never shown as verified, even when nothing the manager touched changed a score: its matches drew from `rngState` in a way the assistant's do not, so its bonus spins cannot be replayed either.
  - a different data stamp or engine: `'unverified'`, with the first draft still resolved for the Draft's ghost chips.

  Nothing throws. Over 200 assistant runs (100 Gauntlets, 100 Cups of 16, seeds `probe-gauntlet-0…` and `probe-cup-0…`) every claim verified `'yes'`; a replay takes about 50 ms (at most 240 ms) in the test suite. Owner's decision applied: only assistant-managed arena runs verify.
- **Opening a link.** `openChallenge` decodes, loads the link's leagues (every pack for a Cup against the champions, the giants or Europe, as its setup does), checks the colours club played that league season, replays, and routes an arena link to `setupMode: 'arena'`, where `App.tsx` shows `ArenaChallengeSetup` (`src/ui/screens/ArenaChallengeSetup.tsx`) in place of `ArenaSetup`: the banner ("Challenge from Minh: Minh climbed 6 rungs, then fell at rung 7 (1-2). Same seed, same wheel, the same ladder…"), the verification line, the challenger's steps as chips, the team name, the summary line and "Take on Minh" (which asks before dropping an unfinished run). `ChallengeSettings` gains `arena?: { claim, auto }`, and `verified` gains `'hand'`; `claim` then mirrors the arena claim (`pts` is the steps won) so every older reader still finds numbers.
- **The ghost.** The Draft header reads "vs Minh (6 rungs)" (a Cup: "lifted it" or "2 rounds won"); the Draft's spin chips work as for a quick link. On the hub the opponent card says "👻 Minh here: 2-1", "👻 Minh fell here: 1-2" or "👻 Minh went no further"; the ladder marks the rung where Minh fell with 👻 and names his score in each rung's tooltip; the Cup's draw tab shows one chip per round above the bracket. The summary repeats the line under every step of the run.
- **Verdict.** Further first, then goal difference over the run, then fewer conceded: `⚔️ Climbed 9 rungs; Minh fell at rung 7`, `⚔️ Minh climbed 9 rungs; you fell at rung 7`, `⚔️ Both climbed 6 rungs; your goal difference was better`, `⚔️ Both lifted it; Minh conceded fewer`, `⚔️ You lifted it; Minh went out in the semi-final`, `⚔️ Both climbed 6 rungs; dead level with Minh`, `⚔️ Both fell at rung 1; Minh conceded fewer` (a run out at the first rung never reads as zero rungs climbed). It closes the share text's lines (after a salary cap's money line) and the card's, and the summary's Head-to-head panel shows it with the verification line.
- **Summary buttons.** "Challenge a friend" copies the link and shows it in a field (its note says whether the run will verify or read as your word); "Run it back" (`runItBackArena`) starts the same seed at once with your own run as the ghost, "your ghost", `'yes'` when the assistant played it and `'hand'` otherwise, the Daily Arena's flag dropped.
- **Best runs.** `ArenaRunEntry.challenge = { name, score }` for a run played from a link; the Profile page shows "vs Minh 6" beside the team, green when beaten, red when not. A toggle (remembered per browser in `touchline-runs-view`) switches between best first and **Best on this ladder** (`groupRunsByLadder`): runs on the same format, size, field and seed under one heading ("Seed K7Q2-MX9A · The Gauntlet · 3 runs · best 7 rungs"), best first.

## 18. Online head-to-head rooms

The hot-seat head-to-head played on two devices (built on `online-rooms`, now in `main`; the server side is in docs/ONLINE.md section 5e). It needs the online layer (a build with `VITE_SUPABASE_*`); without it Head-to-head shows exactly the hot-seat setup.

- **Same game as the hot-seat.** The draft is the engine's own head-to-head (`newH2HDraft`, `h2hSpin`, `h2hPick`, snake order, one player per manager, a manager with no usable player misses the spin), with the hot-seat's rules (`h2hRules` in `src/game/logic/h2h.ts`: the Quick Draft's, no re-spins, no twists), and the season is `runH2HSeason` on the RNG where the draft left it. A room on seed S with the same managers and picks plays exactly the hot-seat game on seed S (`src/game/__tests__/h2hRoom.test.ts` checks squads and the whole result, and `check:verifier` does it again through the server bundle). No draw was added or moved, so rooms needed no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump; `h2hSpin` was split into its two draws and `h2hLand` (everything a spin does after them), which an online client uses to rebuild the draft from the room's log without the seed.
- **Setup.** Head-to-head's head gets a switch, Play with a friend / This device (`h2h-where-online`, `h2h-where-device`; Play with a friend is selected first in an online build, and a Rematch of a hot-seat game opens on This device). Play with a friend: **Make a room** (your name, formation, the club whose place you take, the league, the wheel (this league or all leagues), the season, difficulty, hard mode: the hot-seat's choices for one manager) or **Join with a code** (`K7Q2-MX9A`, any case, dashes or spaces). The rooms this device holds a seat in are listed under it.
- **Waiting room.** The code in large letters, the link (`https://touchline.quest/#h=K7Q2MX9A`) with Copy link, "Waiting for the other manager · closes in 27 min", Close the room. A room nobody joins within 30 minutes closes.
- **Joining.** The link opens the room straight away (on any device, signed in or not). The second manager picks a name (not the first's), a formation and a club (not the first's) and presses Join and start the draft; the server spins at once, up to the first pick.
- **The draft.** The hot-seat's three boxes: the reel and whose turn it is ("Your pick" or "Waiting for Ana"), the squad on the wheel (the Draft button only on your turn), both pitches. Spins are the server's: nobody presses Spin, and each device's reel runs when its poll brings a new spin. A pick goes to the server, which replays the room from its seed, applies the pick with the engine's own checks and spins on to the next pick. A draft with no pick for 15 minutes times out for both; either manager can leave, which ends the room. A third device with the link watches.
- **Result.** The hot-seat's result screen (both sides, derbies, XIs, the table, share text, cards), labelled Online head-to-head, with Rematch for a manager who held a seat (New room for a watcher). Rematch (`RoomSession.rematch`, through the ordinary `room.create`) makes a new room with the same settings and this seat's name, shape and club; the seat record keeps the old code and the other manager's name, so the waiting room and the invite say who it is for, and a second press opens that room while it waits or drafts. The other manager's result has a box for the rematch's code or link, so the two never make a room each. An ended room offers the same. The season is the server's, played the moment the last pick completes both squads; the seed is shown only then. Each device that held a seat files the room once on the Trophy Room's own online shelf (`Cabinet.h2hOnline: { played, won }`, `creditH2HOnline` through the store's `creditH2HRoom(won)`; won when this device's manager finished higher), never on the hot-seat's `h2h` count nor the league and cup totals, so `profile.trophies` is unchanged. Profile shows it as "Online head-to-head" once a room has finished.
- **Fairness.** The seed stays on the server until the room has finished, so nobody can foresee the next spin; the public log holds only where each spin landed and each pick. Every pick is checked by replaying the room, and the turn order is enforced twice (by the replay and by the database).
- **Nothing in the save.** A room is not a `Game` and never enters `touchline-save-v1` or the cloud slots; the device keeps only its seat (`touchline-rooms-v1`, two days).
